# Pull Request Review Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** When a pull request (GitHub) or merge request (GitLab) on a registered repo becomes ready for review, or someone with write access comments `/embry0 review`, embry0 runs a `pr-review` job that reviews the exact head commit in a sandbox and posts an advisory review (summary plus inline comments) on it; a re-review continues the previous review's agent session.

**Architecture:** A pure trigger parser turns forge payloads into `ReviewTrigger`s; a `PrReviewDispatcher` applies the five gates (enabled, not embry0's own, author trust, dedupe through a UNIQUE `pr_reviews.trigger_key`, budget) and asks the `IssueExecutor` for a `pr-review` job. The job runs a new LangGraph pipeline `init → review → publish → cleanup` modelled on `onboard`: init clones the base through the git-proxy and checks out the exact head (fork heads via `refs/pull/<n>/head` / `refs/merge-requests/<iid>/head`), review runs a seeded `pr-reviewer` agent and parses its JSON verdict, publish maps findings to commentable diff lines and posts from the orchestrator with the forge token, cleanup destroys the sandbox.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12, FastAPI, LangGraph, asyncpg/PostgreSQL, httpx (`ForgeClient`), React 19 + TanStack Query + vitest.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-pr-review-design.md` (issue #30). Read it before any task; this plan argues from it.

## Global Constraints

- Purpose: "Advisory comments. It never approves, never requests changes, and sets no status check." GitHub reviews are posted with `event: "COMMENT"` only.
- Triggers: "The transition to ready, or opened directly as non-draft. Pushes do not re-trigger."
- On demand: "A `/embry0 review` comment from someone with write access, on GitHub or GitLab." GitHub: `author_association` OWNER, MEMBER or COLLABORATOR and not a bot (`check_comment_author`). GitLab: project access level Developer (30) or higher via `/projects/:id/members/all/:user_id`.
- The command "is matched as a line prefix, case-insensitively". "A comment carrying only `@embry0` never starts a review, and a `/embry0 review` comment is not dispatched as a follow-up."
- Gates, in this order, each refusal logged with its reason and posting nothing: registered + `pr_review.enabled`; not embry0's own; author write access (commands); dedupe (`ready:<repo>#<number>@<sha>`, `command:<forge>:<comment id>`, UNIQUE index); `BudgetGate.check(actor)` before the job is created.
- Re-review: "Continues the agent session of the pull request's previous review."
- Depth: "A per-repo setting: `full` (default), `targeted` or `read_only`." "Forks: Always reviewed read-only, whatever the depth."
- Model: "The deployment's heavy tier, with a per-repo override." Default: "Off per repo, until an operator turns it on."
- Limits: "each check gets 20 minutes, and the agent run gets 60 minutes." Changed-file list "capped at 200 (`format_review_scope`)".
- Publishing "runs in the orchestrator, with the forge token from `ForgeClient.token_for`. Nothing is posted from the sandbox."
- "Inline comments are capped at 30, worst severity first, and the rest are listed in the summary. Each inline comment starts with **Critical**, **Important** or **Suggestion**."
- Summary ends with the hidden marker `<!-- embry0-review job=<job_id> sha=<sha> -->`.
- Storage: "One migration, numbered `max + 1` when it merges." Today's max is 94, so this plan uses **95**; renumber to max+1 at merge time and move the `test_migration_<n>_is_the_last_entry` assertion with it.
- "There are no new routes, so what each ingress publishes is unchanged." No sandbox image rebuild; no proxy change (the git-proxy forwards `git-upload-pack` unfiltered, so the fork refs are fetchable; see `embry0/execution/proxy/git_proxy.py::_git_handler`).
- Vendor-neutral naming (CONTRIBUTING.md): fixtures use `acme/widgets`, `acme/products/web`, `gitlab.example.com`, `octocat`, `jdoe`. No customer, deployment, host or person names anywhere.
- Every new test is shown failing first with its own assertion message. Read the pytest summary line, never only `grep FAILED`.
- Commits follow `.claude/skills/commit-standard/SKILL.md`: Conventional Commits, subject ≤ 72 chars, body wrapped at 72, no AI co-author footer, last line `Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_01Q5pSvJSg6T12CagYZMAHYy`.
- Work only in `/Users/tomas/repos/embry0-worktrees/pr-review` on `feat/pr-review`. Every command below runs from that directory. The worktree has no `.venv`: Python commands use `PYTHONPATH=. /Users/tomas/repos/embry0/.venv/bin/python` (verified: `embry0.__file__` then resolves to the worktree). If a later `uv sync` creates `.venv` in the worktree, use `.venv/bin/python` instead.
- Backend code is `mypy --strict` clean and `ruff check` / `ruff format --check` clean.
- Before every commit step, run `PYTHONPATH=. /Users/tomas/repos/embry0/.venv/bin/python -m ruff format <the .py files the task touched>` and `PYTHONPATH=. /Users/tomas/repos/embry0/.venv/bin/python -m ruff check --fix <same files>` (the import blocks in this plan are not always in ruff's order); code in this plan is written for readability and the formatter decides line breaks. Re-run the task's tests after formatting.

## Review Focus

1. **Older GitLab payloads that carry `work_in_progress` and no `draft`**, in `object_attributes` and in `changes`: a reasonable operator expects "marked ready" to trigger exactly as on a current GitLab, and an MR opened as WIP not to. Test: Task 2 `test_gitlab_legacy_work_in_progress_*`.
2. **A head that moved or was force-pushed between the trigger and the run, or between the review and the publish.** Expected: an unreachable head fails the job with a message saying so and posts nothing; a previous reviewed sha that is no longer an ancestor gets the "branch was rewritten" prompt, not a bogus delta; if the PR head moved before publishing, nothing is commented inline against stale lines (every finding goes into the summary); GitHub's retry after a 422 drops `commit_id`. Tests: Task 8 `test_head_that_is_gone_exits_3_with_a_message`, `test_a_rewritten_branch_reports_delta_rewritten`; Task 7 `test_head_moved_since_review_posts_everything_in_the_summary` (both forges), `test_a_422_retries_once_without_comments_or_commit_id`.
3. **Binary, renamed, deleted and patch-less files.** GitHub omits `patch` for binary and very large files; GitLab sends `""` or `Binary files … differ`. Expected: no inline comment is attempted on them (no 422 storm), findings on them land in the summary, renamed files are addressed by their new path with the old path kept for GitLab positions. Test: Task 5 `test_binary_and_patchless_files_are_not_commentable`, `test_renamed_file_is_keyed_by_new_path`.
4. **`/embry0 review` that is not a request**: inside a fenced code block, in a `>` quote, as `/embry0 reviewer`, or in embry0's own summary or inline comment (which would loop on GitLab, where embry0's notes come back through the Note Hook with a Developer+ access level). Expected: no review. Tests: Task 2 `test_command_inside_code_fence_or_quote_is_ignored`, `test_our_own_comment_never_triggers`; Task 6 `test_the_summary_never_reads_as_a_command`.
5. **Findings whose path or line does not match the forge's spelling**: `./src/a.py`, `/workspace/src/a.py`, `b/src/a.py`, a line of `0`, `-3`, `"42"`, `true`, missing, or just outside a hunk. Expected: the path is normalised, a numeric string is read, everything unplaceable goes to "Elsewhere in the code" with its `file:line`, and nothing is sent that the forge would refuse. Tests: Task 6 `test_line_values_are_read_or_dropped`, `test_paths_are_normalised_before_placement`, `test_a_line_just_outside_a_hunk_goes_elsewhere`.

## Where this plan settles what the spec leaves open

Each line is a reading of the code that the tasks rely on; a reviewer who disagrees should say so before Task 1.

- **GitLab routing.** `api/v1/webhooks.py::gitlab_webhook` has no `object_kind` dispatch: it hands every delivery to `GitLabSyncService.handle_webhook_event`, which drops non-issue kinds. Task 4 routes `merge_request` and `note` to the reviewer in the route itself, before the sync, and leaves issues on the sync.
- **No proxy change.** The git-proxy forwards `git-upload-pack` without inspecting refs (`git_proxy.py::_git_handler`), so `refs/pull/<n>/head` and `refs/merge-requests/<iid>/head` fetch through it today. No proxy image rebuild.
- **Verdict parsing.** `_review_payload`'s last-object fallback looks for a `decision` key, which the review verdict has no reason to carry; `parse_verdict` adds a `summary`-keyed fallback after it (Task 6).
- **Profile.** The builtin `slim` profile already joins `sandbox-internet`, so it is the default for every depth; "a profile with internet egress" needs no second default.
- **`pr_url`.** A review job never stores `jobs.pr_url`: gate 2 ("no job has it as `pr_url`") and merge sync both read that column, and a review is not the pull request's author. Gate 2 also ignores `pr-followup` jobs, which carry the URL of a pull request someone else opened.
- **Verdict storage.** `pr_reviews` gains a `verdict` column beyond the spec's list, so a re-review without a session can quote the earlier findings, also after the earlier job was purged, and so "the verdict stays in the job's record" holds whether or not it was posted.
- **Fork detection.** `PullRequestFact.from_fork` treats a missing `head.repo` key as same-repo; the trigger parser computes fork-ness itself and treats an unknown or deleted head repository as a fork (read-only).
- **GitLab actor.** The Merge Request Hook names the author by id only, so a GitLab ready event charges `user.username` (whoever opened it or marked it ready).
- **Manual trigger.** `embry0 review` skips the enabled setting but still requires a registered repo (the executor refuses unregistered repos everywhere, #360). It is a `command` trigger keyed `command:<forge>:manual-<hex>`, so it continues the previous review's session.
- **Targeted depth with no `dev_checks`.** Discovery is for `full` only; `targeted` with nothing configured reviews by reading.
- **Read-only depth.** The agent gets no `Bash` tool, and the diff is precomputed into `/workspace/.git/embry0-review/`, inside the Ring-2 `/workspace/**` allow-list and where no pull request can plant a file.
- **Head moved before publish.** If the pull request's head is no longer the reviewed sha when publishing, nothing is placed inline (the lines belong to another commit) and every finding goes into the summary. GitHub's 422 retry drops `commit_id` as well as the inline comments.
- **Self-trigger.** embry0's own GitLab notes come back through the Note Hook from a Developer+ user; every comment embry0 posts carries the `<!-- embry0-review` marker, and a body carrying it never triggers.
- **Workflow registry.** `WorkflowRegistry` (app.py) registers only issue-to-pr; onboard is not in it, and pr-review follows onboard.

## Before Task 1: a throwaway Postgres for the DB tests

Database tests must hit a throwaway server, never a deployment's database (`db_with_migrations` runs `DROP SCHEMA public CASCADE`). Shell state does not persist between commands, so the URL is kept in a 0600 file.

- [ ] **Step 1: Start the server and create this plan's database**

```bash
PW=$(python3 -c 'import secrets; print(secrets.token_urlsafe(24))')
POSTGRES_PASSWORD="$PW" docker run -d --name embry0-test-pg-pr-review \
  --tmpfs /var/lib/postgresql/data -p 127.0.0.1:55433:5432 \
  -e POSTGRES_USER=embry0 -e POSTGRES_PASSWORD \
  postgres:16.6-alpine -c fsync=off -c synchronous_commit=off -c full_page_writes=off
until docker exec embry0-test-pg-pr-review pg_isready -h 127.0.0.1 -U embry0 -q; do sleep 1; done
docker exec embry0-test-pg-pr-review createdb -U embry0 pr_review_plan
umask 077; echo "postgresql://embry0:$PW@127.0.0.1:55433/pr_review_plan" > /tmp/embry0-pr-review-testdb.url
```

Expected: a container id, then no output. Every DB test command below starts with `TEST_DATABASE_URL="$(cat /tmp/embry0-pr-review-testdb.url)"`. Task 13 removes the container.

If a connection to `127.0.0.1:55433` hangs (seen on this Mac while planning: the OrbStack port forward accepted TCP but never answered the Postgres handshake, and the Claude Code sandbox also blocks local TCP, so run these commands with the sandbox disabled), use a native throwaway cluster instead; Homebrew's `postgresql@14` binaries are on the PATH:

```bash
docker rm -f embry0-test-pg-pr-review
D=$(mktemp -d); mkdir -p /tmp/e0pg   # socket dir: macOS caps socket paths at 103 bytes
initdb -D "$D" -U embry0 --auth=trust -E UTF8 >/dev/null
pg_ctl -D "$D" -o "-p 55433 -k /tmp/e0pg -c listen_addresses=127.0.0.1 -c fsync=off -c max_connections=200" -l "$D/pg.log" start
createdb -h 127.0.0.1 -p 55433 -U embry0 pr_review_plan
umask 077; echo "postgresql://embry0@127.0.0.1:55433/pr_review_plan" > /tmp/embry0-pr-review-testdb.url; echo "$D" > /tmp/embry0-pr-review-pgdata
```

With this cluster, Task 13 runs `createdb`/`dropdb -h 127.0.0.1 -p 55433 -U embry0 <name>` in place of `docker exec … createdb/dropdb`, and stops it with `pg_ctl -D "$(cat /tmp/embry0-pr-review-pgdata)" stop` in place of `docker rm -f`.

### Task 0: Let the spec and the plan ship under `docs/superpowers/`

`tests/unit/docs/test_markdown_links.py::test_internal_material_is_not_under_docs` (#529) fails any tree with a file under `docs/superpowers/`, while CLAUDE.md (#21, kept by #27) says "Specs and plans from the superpowers skills go in `docs/superpowers/specs/` and `docs/superpowers/plans/`, committed on the feature branch". This branch already carries the spec there, so the full suite fails on it today, before any code (measured while planning: `1 failed` at that test in an otherwise passing run). The test predates the rule; the rule is the newer decision. Fix the test, do not move the spec.

This is a pre-existing bug that blocks the task, so it is fixed here and also filed.

Expected: `1 failed` — `AssertionError: internal material under docs/ (move it to internal/): ['docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-pr-review-design.md']`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Narrow the guard to what is still internal**

Replace the test with:

```python
def test_internal_material_is_not_under_docs():
    """#529: audits and RCAs live under internal/, which the public export drops.

    Specs and plans are different since #21: they ship in the pull request of
    their work, under docs/superpowers/specs/ and docs/superpowers/plans/
    (CLAUDE.md). Anything else under docs/superpowers/ is still refused.
    """
    misplaced = []
    for p in _markdown_files(ROOT):
        parts = p.relative_to(ROOT).parts
        if parts[:2] in {("docs", "audits"), ("docs", "rca")}:
            misplaced.append(str(p.relative_to(ROOT)))
        elif parts[:2] == ("docs", "superpowers") and parts[2:3] not in {("specs",), ("plans",)}:
            misplaced.append(str(p.relative_to(ROOT)))
    assert not misplaced, f"internal material under docs/ (move it to internal/): {misplaced}"
    # The public export has no internal/ at all; where it exists, its README
    # says what belongs there.
    internal = ROOT / "internal"
    assert not internal.exists() or (internal / "README.md").is_file(), "internal/ has no README.md"


def test_a_stray_superpowers_document_is_still_refused(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    stray = tmp_path / "docs" / "superpowers" / "notes" / "x.md"
    stray.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    stray.write_text("# x\n")
    monkeypatch.setattr(sys.modules[__name__], "ROOT", tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError, match="docs/superpowers/notes/x.md"):
        test_internal_material_is_not_under_docs()
```

The module imports neither `sys` nor `pytest`: add `import sys` after `import subprocess` and `import pytest` after the standard-library block. `_markdown_files(root)` falls back to `rglob` when `root` has no `.git`, which is what the stray-document test relies on.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing migration test**

Create `tests/unit/storage/test_migration_95.py`:

```python
"""Migration 95 — pr_reviews and repo_preferences.pr_review (#30)."""

import asyncpg
import pytest

from embry0.storage.database import DatabasePool
from embry0.storage.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS

SHA = "a" * 40


def _sql_for(version: int) -> str:
    match = [sql for v, _, sql in MIGRATIONS if v == version]
    assert len(match) == 1, f"migration {version} must exist exactly once"
    return match[0]


def test_migration_95_present_and_gapless() -> None:
    versions = [v for v, _, _ in MIGRATIONS]
    assert 95 in versions, "migration 95 (pr_reviews, #30) is missing"
    assert versions == list(range(1, len(versions) + 1))


def test_migration_95_is_the_last_entry() -> None:
    """The newest migration owns this assertion; it passes to the next one
    (it came from tests/unit/storage/test_migration_94.py)."""
    assert MIGRATIONS[-1][0] == 95, "migration 95 must add pr_reviews"


@pytest.mark.requires_postgres
@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_a_trigger_key_is_stored_once(db_with_migrations: DatabasePool) -> None:
    db = db_with_migrations
    await db.execute(_sql_for(95))  # a second run must be a no-op
    insert = (
        "INSERT INTO pr_reviews (id, repo, number, head_sha, trigger, trigger_key) "
        "VALUES ($1, 'acme/widgets', 7, $2, 'ready', $3)"
    )
    key = f"ready:acme/widgets#7@{SHA}"
    await db.execute(insert, "prr-1", SHA, key)
    with pytest.raises(asyncpg.UniqueViolationError):
        await db.execute(insert, "prr-2", SHA, key)


@pytest.mark.requires_postgres
@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_the_trigger_takes_the_vocabulary_and_nothing_else(db_with_migrations: DatabasePool) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(asyncpg.CheckViolationError):
        await db_with_migrations.execute(
            "INSERT INTO pr_reviews (id, repo, number, head_sha, trigger, trigger_key) "
            "VALUES ('prr-x', 'acme/widgets', 7, 'abc1234', 'push', 'push:1')"
        )


@pytest.mark.requires_postgres
@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_repo_preferences_gains_a_nullable_pr_review_column(db_with_migrations: DatabasePool) -> None:
    row = await db_with_migrations.fetchrow(
        "SELECT data_type, is_nullable FROM information_schema.columns "
        "WHERE table_name = 'repo_preferences' AND column_name = 'pr_review'"
    )
    assert row is not None, "repo_preferences.pr_review is missing"
    assert (row["data_type"], row["is_nullable"]) == ("jsonb", "YES")
```

In `embry0/storage/migrations/runner.py`, after the `(94, ...)` tuple and before the `]` that closes `MIGRATIONS`, add:

```python
    (
        95,
        "pr_reviews + repo_preferences.pr_review — review a pull request when it is ready (#30)",
        # One row per review a trigger asked for. trigger_key is the dedupe
        # key — ready:<repo>#<number>@<sha> for a ready event,
        # command:<forge>:<comment id> for a /embry0 review comment — and the
        # unique index makes a redelivered webhook a no-op across restarts.
        # job_id is SET NULL when a job row is purged, so the earlier review's
        # verdict outlives it and the next review can still read it.
        """
        ALTER TABLE repo_preferences ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pr_review JSONB;
        COMMENT ON COLUMN repo_preferences.pr_review IS
            '#30: pull request review settings {"enabled": bool, "depth": "full"|"targeted"|"read_only", "model": str|null}. NULL = never configured, which means off.';

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pr_reviews (
            id          TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            repo        TEXT NOT NULL,
            number      INTEGER NOT NULL,
            head_sha    TEXT NOT NULL,
            trigger     TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (trigger IN ('ready', 'command')),
            trigger_key TEXT NOT NULL,
            job_id      TEXT REFERENCES jobs(job_id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
            verdict     JSONB,
            created_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
            posted_at   TIMESTAMPTZ
        );
        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pr_reviews_trigger_key ON pr_reviews (trigger_key);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pr_reviews_repo_number ON pr_reviews (repo, number, created_at DESC);
        COMMENT ON COLUMN pr_reviews.verdict IS
            '#30: the reviewer''s parsed verdict, kept whether or not it was posted. The next review of the same pull request reads it.';
        COMMENT ON COLUMN pr_reviews.posted_at IS
            '#30: when the review landed on the forge. NULL = not (yet) posted.';
        """,
    ),
```

`verdict` is one column more than the spec's list: the spec requires that "the verdict stays in the job's record" and that a re-review without a session falls back to "the previous review's findings as text", including after the earlier job was deleted. Neither is possible without storing it here.

"""PrReviewsRepository against real Postgres (#30)."""

import pytest

from embry0.storage.database import DatabasePool
from embry0.storage.repositories.jobs import JobsRepository
from embry0.storage.repositories.pr_reviews import PrReviewsRepository

pytestmark = [pytest.mark.requires_postgres, pytest.mark.asyncio]

SHA1 = "1" * 40
SHA2 = "2" * 40
VERDICT = {"summary": "Looks careful.", "review_comments": []}


async def _claim(reviews: PrReviewsRepository, key: str, sha: str = SHA1, number: int = 7) -> str:
    review_id = await reviews.claim(repo="acme/widgets", number=number, head_sha=sha, trigger="ready", trigger_key=key)
    assert review_id is not None, f"claim of a fresh key {key!r} returned None"
    return review_id


async def test_a_key_is_claimed_once(db_with_migrations: DatabasePool) -> None:
    reviews = PrReviewsRepository(db_with_migrations)
    key = f"ready:acme/widgets#7@{SHA1}"

    first = await _claim(reviews, key)
    second = await reviews.claim(repo="acme/widgets", number=7, head_sha=SHA1, trigger="ready", trigger_key=key)

    assert first.startswith("prr-")
    assert second is None, "a redelivered trigger must not claim a second review"
    assert await reviews.exists(key) is True
    assert await reviews.exists(f"ready:acme/widgets#7@{SHA2}") is False


async def test_a_released_claim_can_be_taken_again(db_with_migrations: DatabasePool) -> None:
    reviews = PrReviewsRepository(db_with_migrations)
    key = "command:github:1001"
    review_id = await _claim(reviews, key)

    await reviews.release(review_id)

    assert await reviews.exists(key) is False, "release() left the claim in place"
    assert await _claim(reviews, key)


async def test_release_never_drops_a_review_that_has_a_job(db_with_migrations: DatabasePool) -> None:
    reviews = PrReviewsRepository(db_with_migrations)
    job_id = await JobsRepository(db_with_migrations).create(repo="acme/widgets", task="t", dispatched_by="octocat")
    review_id = await _claim(reviews, "command:github:1002")
    await reviews.attach_job(review_id, job_id)

    await reviews.release(review_id)

    row = await reviews.get(review_id)
    assert row is not None and row["job_id"] == job_id, "release() deleted a review a job is running"


async def test_outcome_keeps_the_verdict_and_stamps_posted_at_only_when_posted(db_with_migrations: DatabasePool) -> None:
    reviews = PrReviewsRepository(db_with_migrations)
    unposted = await _claim(reviews, "command:github:1")
    posted = await _claim(reviews, "command:github:2")

    await reviews.record_outcome(unposted, verdict=VERDICT, posted=False)
    await reviews.record_outcome(posted, verdict=VERDICT, posted=True)

    row_unposted, row_posted = await reviews.get(unposted), await reviews.get(posted)
    assert row_unposted is not None and row_posted is not None
    assert row_unposted["verdict"] == VERDICT and row_unposted["posted_at"] is None
    assert row_posted["verdict"] == VERDICT and row_posted["posted_at"] is not None


async def test_previous_reviews_are_the_posted_ones_newest_first_without_this_job(db_with_migrations: DatabasePool) -> None:
    jobs = JobsRepository(db_with_migrations)
    reviews = PrReviewsRepository(db_with_migrations)
    job_ids = [await jobs.create(repo="acme/widgets", task="t", dispatched_by="octocat") for _ in range(3)]
    for index, (job_id, sha) in enumerate(zip(job_ids, [SHA1, SHA2, SHA2], strict=True)):
        review_id = await _claim(reviews, f"command:github:{index}", sha=sha)
        await reviews.attach_job(review_id, job_id)
        if index < 2:
            await reviews.record_outcome(review_id, verdict=VERDICT, posted=True)
    await _claim(reviews, "command:github:other-pr", number=8)

    rows = await reviews.previous_reviews("acme/widgets", 7, exclude_job_id=job_ids[2])
    assert [r["job_id"] for r in rows] == [job_ids[1], job_ids[0]], "expected the posted reviews, newest first"
    assert [r["head_sha"] for r in rows] == [SHA2, SHA1]

    assert [r["job_id"] for r in await reviews.previous_reviews("acme/widgets", 7, exclude_job_id=job_ids[1])] == [
        job_ids[0]
    ], "a review must never be its own previous review"
    assert await reviews.previous_reviews("acme/widgets", 9, exclude_job_id="job-x") == []


async def test_a_purged_job_leaves_its_review_and_verdict(db_with_migrations: DatabasePool) -> None:
    jobs = JobsRepository(db_with_migrations)
    reviews = PrReviewsRepository(db_with_migrations)
    job_id = await jobs.create(repo="acme/widgets", task="t", dispatched_by="octocat")
    review_id = await _claim(reviews, "command:github:9")
    await reviews.attach_job(review_id, job_id)
    await reviews.record_outcome(review_id, verdict=VERDICT, posted=True)

    await db_with_migrations.execute("DELETE FROM jobs WHERE job_id = $1", job_id)

    rows = await reviews.previous_reviews("acme/widgets", 7, exclude_job_id="job-new")
    assert len(rows) == 1 and rows[0]["job_id"] is None and rows[0]["verdict"] == VERDICT

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement the repository**

Create `embry0/storage/repositories/pr_reviews.py`:

```python
"""Pull request reviews (#30): one row per review a trigger asked for.

``trigger_key`` is UNIQUE, so a redelivered webhook — before or after a
restart — claims nothing the second time. A row outlives its job (``job_id``
is SET NULL when the job is purged): the next review of the same pull request
reads the earlier verdict from here.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import uuid
from typing import Any

from embry0.storage.database import DatabasePool

_COLUMNS = "id, repo, number, head_sha, trigger, trigger_key, job_id, verdict, created_at, posted_at"


class PrReviewsRepository:
    """CRUD for ``pr_reviews`` (migration 95)."""

    def __init__(self, db: DatabasePool) -> None:
        self._db = db

    async def exists(self, trigger_key: str) -> bool:
        return bool(await self._db.fetchval("SELECT 1 FROM pr_reviews WHERE trigger_key = $1", trigger_key))

    async def claim(self, *, repo: str, number: int, head_sha: str, trigger: str, trigger_key: str) -> str | None:
        """Record the review. Its id, or None when ``trigger_key`` was already claimed."""
        review_id = f"prr-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
        row = await self._db.fetchrow(
            """
            INSERT INTO pr_reviews (id, repo, number, head_sha, trigger, trigger_key)
            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)
            ON CONFLICT (trigger_key) DO NOTHING
            RETURNING id
            """,
            review_id,
            repo,
            number,
            head_sha,
            trigger,
            trigger_key,
        )
        return str(row["id"]) if row else None

    async def attach_job(self, review_id: str, job_id: str) -> None:
        await self._db.execute("UPDATE pr_reviews SET job_id = $2 WHERE id = $1", review_id, job_id)

    async def release(self, review_id: str) -> None:
        """Give a claim back when its job could not be created. A review with a job is kept."""
        await self._db.execute("DELETE FROM pr_reviews WHERE id = $1 AND job_id IS NULL", review_id)

    async def record_outcome(self, review_id: str, *, verdict: dict[str, Any] | None, posted: bool) -> None:
        await self._db.execute(
            "UPDATE pr_reviews SET verdict = $2::jsonb, "
            "posted_at = CASE WHEN $3::boolean THEN NOW() ELSE posted_at END WHERE id = $1",
            review_id,
            verdict,
            posted,
        )

    async def get(self, review_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        row = await self._db.fetchrow(f"SELECT {_COLUMNS} FROM pr_reviews WHERE id = $1", review_id)
        return dict(row) if row else None

    async def previous_reviews(
        self, repo: str, number: int, *, exclude_job_id: str, limit: int = 5
    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Posted reviews of this pull request, newest first, never ``exclude_job_id``'s own."""
        rows = await self._db.fetch(
            f"SELECT {_COLUMNS} FROM pr_reviews "
            "WHERE repo = $1 AND number = $2 AND posted_at IS NOT NULL AND job_id IS DISTINCT FROM $3 "
            "ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT $4",
            repo,
            number,
            exclude_job_id,
            limit,
        )
        return [dict(r) for r in rows]
```

```python
class PrReviewSettings(BaseModel):
    """#30: per-repo pull request review. Off until an operator turns it on."""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    # Strict: pydantic would read "yes" as true, and a typo must not turn reviews on.
    enabled: StrictBool = False
    depth: Literal["full", "targeted", "read_only"] = "full"
    # None = the deployment's heavy tier (MODEL_HEAVY).
    model: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=200)

    @field_validator("model")
    @classmethod
    def _blank_is_the_default(cls, v: str | None) -> str | None:
        if v is None:
            return None
        return v.strip() or None

    if req.pr_review is not None and req.pr_review.model:
        from embry0.services.model_enablement import UnpricedModelError, ensure_models_priced

        try:
            ensure_models_priced([req.pr_review.model])
        except UnpricedModelError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=str(exc)) from exc

    prefs_repo = request.app.state.repo_preferences_repo
    # #350: only the fields the client sent are written; an omitted field
    # keeps its stored value, an explicit null (or "", [] or {}) clears it.
    # exclude_unset also keeps verification {} as {} (= clear) rather than
    # a dict of defaults.
    fields = req.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)
    if req.pr_review is not None:
        # #30: exclude_unset reaches into nested models; the settings are one
        # object and are stored whole, defaults included.
        fields["pr_review"] = req.pr_review.model_dump()
    saved = await prefs_repo.upsert(full_repo, **fields)
    return RepoPreferencesResponse(**saved)

Append to `tests/fixtures/webhooks/payloads.py`, in the GitHub section after `github_pull_request`:

```python
def github_review_pull_request(
    *,
    action: str = "ready_for_review",
    number: int = 7,
    repo: str = "acme/widgets",
    head_repo: str | None = "acme/widgets",
    head_ref: str = "feature/login",
    head_sha: str = "a" * 40,
    base_ref: str = "main",
    draft: bool | None = False,
    author: str = "octocat",
    state: str = "open",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """A `pull_request` delivery with every field the reviewer reads (#30).

    ``head_repo=None`` is a fork whose repository was deleted: GitHub sends
    ``head.repo: null``. ``draft=None`` leaves the key out.
    """
    pr: dict[str, Any] = {
        "number": number,
        "html_url": f"https://github.com/{repo}/pull/{number}",
        "title": "Add the login form",
        "body": "Adds the login form and its validation.",
        "state": state,
        "user": {"login": author},
        "head": {"ref": head_ref, "sha": head_sha, "repo": {"full_name": head_repo} if head_repo else None},
        "base": {"ref": base_ref, "sha": "b" * 40, "repo": {"full_name": repo}},
        "updated_at": "2026-10-15T10:00:00Z",
    }
    if draft is not None:
        pr["draft"] = draft
    return {"action": action, "repository": {"full_name": repo}, "sender": {"login": author}, "pull_request": pr}
```

In the GitLab section after `gitlab_issue`:

```python
def gitlab_merge_request(
    *,
    action: str = "update",
    iid: int = 5,
    project_path: str = "acme/products/web",
    draft: bool | None = False,
    previous_draft: bool | None = True,
    legacy_wip: bool = False,
    source_project_id: int = 1,
    target_project_id: int = 1,
    sha: str = "c" * 40,
    state: str = "opened",
    username: str = "jdoe",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """GitLab Merge Request Hook (#30).

    ``legacy_wip`` spells the draft flag ``work_in_progress``, as GitLab did
    before ``draft`` existed, in ``object_attributes`` and in ``changes``.
    ``previous_draft=None`` sends no ``changes`` delta at all.
    """
    key = "work_in_progress" if legacy_wip else "draft"
    attrs: dict[str, Any] = {
        "iid": iid,
        "title": "Add search",
        "description": "Adds search to the catalogue.",
        "action": action,
        "state": state,
        "url": f"https://gitlab.example.com/{project_path}/-/merge_requests/{iid}",
        "source_branch": "feature/search",
        "target_branch": "main",
        "source_project_id": source_project_id,
        "target_project_id": target_project_id,
        "last_commit": {"id": sha},
    }
    if draft is not None:
        attrs[key] = draft
    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
        "object_kind": "merge_request",
        "event_type": "merge_request",
        "user": {"id": 42, "username": username},
        "project": {"id": target_project_id, "path_with_namespace": project_path},
        "object_attributes": attrs,
    }
    if previous_draft is not None and draft is not None:
        payload["changes"] = {key: {"previous": previous_draft, "current": draft}}
    return payload


def gitlab_mr_note(
    *,
    note: str = "/embry0 review",
    note_id: int = 901,
    iid: int = 5,
    project_path: str = "acme/products/web",
    user_id: int = 42,
    username: str = "jdoe",
    system: bool = False,
    noteable_type: str = "MergeRequest",
    source_project_id: int = 1,
    target_project_id: int = 1,
    sha: str = "c" * 40,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """GitLab Note Hook on a merge request (#30)."""
    return {
        "object_kind": "note",
        "event_type": "note",
        "user": {"id": user_id, "username": username},
        "project": {"id": target_project_id, "path_with_namespace": project_path},
        "object_attributes": {"id": note_id, "note": note, "noteable_type": noteable_type, "system": system},
        "merge_request": {
            "iid": iid,
            "title": "Add search",
            "description": "Adds search to the catalogue.",
            "state": "opened",
            "url": f"https://gitlab.example.com/{project_path}/-/merge_requests/{iid}",
            "source_branch": "feature/search",
            "target_branch": "main",
            "source_project_id": source_project_id,
            "target_project_id": target_project_id,
            "last_commit": {"id": sha},
        },
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the parser**

Create `embry0/services/pr_review/__init__.py`:

```python
"""Pull request review: triggers, gates and dispatch (#30)."""
```

Create `embry0/services/pr_review/triggers.py`:

```python
"""Which forge deliveries ask embry0 to review a pull request (#30).

Pure: no I/O. Two kinds of trigger:

* **ready** — a pull request becomes ready for review (GitHub
  ``ready_for_review``, or ``opened`` with ``draft: false``; GitLab's draft
  flag going true → false, or ``open`` as non-draft). Pushes never trigger.
* **command** — a comment with a line that starts with ``/embry0 review``.
  It is a different trigger from the ``@embry0`` follow-up tag: a comment
  carrying only ``@embry0`` never asks for a review.

GitLab named the draft flag ``work_in_progress`` before ``draft`` existed;
both spellings are read, in ``object_attributes`` and in ``changes``.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Literal

from embry0.trackers.models import actor_of

TriggerKind = Literal["ready", "command"]

#: Every comment embry0 posts carries this, so its own words never ask it
#: for a review — on GitLab its notes come back through the Note Hook from a
#: user with Developer access.
REVIEW_MARKER = "<!-- embry0-review"
EMBRY0_BRANCH_PREFIX = "embry0/"

_SHA_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{7,64}$")
# Up to three leading spaces: four make a markdown code block.
_COMMAND_RE = re.compile(r"^ {0,3}/embry0[ \t]+review(?:\s|$)", re.IGNORECASE)
_FENCE_RE = re.compile(r"^ {0,3}(```|~~~)")
_DRAFT_KEYS = ("draft", "work_in_progress")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PullRequestInfo:
    """One pull request (GitHub) or merge request (GitLab) as the trigger saw it."""

    forge: str
    repo: str
    number: int
    url: str
    title: str
    body: str
    author: str
    head_ref: str
    head_sha: str
    base_ref: str
    from_fork: bool
    is_open: bool
    draft: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReviewTrigger:
    pr: PullRequestInfo
    trigger: TriggerKind
    trigger_key: str
    actor: str  # whom the budget gate charges


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReviewCommand:
    """A ``/embry0 review`` comment, before its pull request is resolved."""

    forge: str
    repo: str
    number: int
    comment_id: str
    author: str
    github_comment: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None
    gitlab_user_id: int | None = None
    gitlab_mr: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None


def ready_key(repo: str, number: int, head_sha: str) -> str:
    return f"ready:{repo}#{number}@{head_sha}"


def command_key(forge: str, comment_id: str) -> str:
    return f"command:{forge}:{comment_id}"


def is_embry0_branch(head_ref: str) -> bool:
    return head_ref.startswith(EMBRY0_BRANCH_PREFIX)


def has_review_command(body: str) -> bool:
    """True when a line of ``body`` starts with ``/embry0 review``.

    Lines inside a fenced code block or a ``>`` quote are someone showing the
    command, not asking for it. A body carrying :data:`REVIEW_MARKER` is
    embry0's own and never counts.
    """
    if not body or REVIEW_MARKER in body:
        return False
    fence: str | None = None
    for line in body.splitlines():
        match = _FENCE_RE.match(line)
        if match:
            if fence is None:
                fence = match.group(1)
            elif match.group(1) == fence:
                fence = None
            continue
        if fence is not None or line.lstrip().startswith(">"):
            continue
        if _COMMAND_RE.match(line):
            return True
    return False


def _str(value: Any) -> str:
    return value if isinstance(value, str) else ""


def _map(value: Any) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    return value if isinstance(value, Mapping) else {}


def _int(value: Any) -> int | None:
    return value if isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool) else None


def _sha(value: Any) -> str | None:
    sha = _str(value).strip().lower()
    return sha if _SHA_RE.match(sha) else None


def pr_from_github(pr: Mapping[str, Any], *, repo: str) -> PullRequestInfo | None:
    """A GitHub pull-request object (webhook or ``GET /pulls/{n}``); None when unusable.

    A fork is any head repository other than the base, including a deleted
    one (``head.repo: null``): unknown means untrusted, so it is reviewed
    read-only.
    """
    number = _int(pr.get("number"))
    url = _str(pr.get("html_url"))
    head = _map(pr.get("head"))
    base = _map(pr.get("base"))
    sha = _sha(head.get("sha"))
    base_ref = _str(base.get("ref"))
    if number is None or not url or sha is None or not base_ref or not repo:
        return None
    head_repo = _str(_map(head.get("repo")).get("full_name"))
    base_repo = _str(_map(base.get("repo")).get("full_name")) or repo
    user = _map(pr.get("user"))
    return PullRequestInfo(
        forge="github",
        repo=repo,
        number=number,
        url=url,
        title=_str(pr.get("title")),
        body=_str(pr.get("body")),
        author=_str(user.get("login")),
        head_ref=_str(head.get("ref")),
        head_sha=sha,
        base_ref=base_ref,
        from_fork=not head_repo or head_repo.lower() != base_repo.lower(),
        is_open=_str(pr.get("state")) == "open",
        draft=pr.get("draft") is True,
    )


def _gitlab_draft(attrs: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bool:
    for key in _DRAFT_KEYS:
        value = attrs.get(key)
        if isinstance(value, bool):
            return value
    return False


def pr_from_gitlab(mr: Mapping[str, Any], *, repo: str) -> PullRequestInfo | None:
    """A GitLab merge request (hook ``object_attributes``, a note's
    ``merge_request``, or ``GET /merge_requests/:iid``); None when unusable."""
    iid = _int(mr.get("iid"))
    url = _str(mr.get("url")) or _str(mr.get("web_url"))
    last_commit = _map(mr.get("last_commit"))
    sha = _sha(last_commit.get("id")) or _sha(mr.get("sha"))
    base_ref = _str(mr.get("target_branch"))
    if iid is None or not url or sha is None or not base_ref or not repo:
        return None
    source, target = _int(mr.get("source_project_id")), _int(mr.get("target_project_id"))
    author = _map(mr.get("author"))
    return PullRequestInfo(
        forge="gitlab",
        repo=repo,
        number=iid,
        url=url,
        title=_str(mr.get("title")),
        body=_str(mr.get("description")),
        author=_str(author.get("username")),
        head_ref=_str(mr.get("source_branch")),
        head_sha=sha,
        base_ref=base_ref,
        from_fork=source is None or target is None or source != target,
        is_open=_str(mr.get("state")) == "opened",
        draft=_gitlab_draft(mr),
    )


def ready_trigger_from_github(payload: Mapping[str, Any], action: str) -> ReviewTrigger | None:
    if action not in ("ready_for_review", "opened"):
        return None
    pr_obj = payload.get("pull_request")
    repository = _map(payload.get("repository"))
    repo = _str(repository.get("full_name"))
    if not isinstance(pr_obj, Mapping) or not repo:
        return None
    # Only an explicit `draft: false` makes `opened` a ready event.
    if action == "opened" and pr_obj.get("draft") is not False:
        return None
    pr = pr_from_github(pr_obj, repo=repo)
    if pr is None or not pr.is_open:
        return None
    return ReviewTrigger(
        pr=pr,
        trigger="ready",
        trigger_key=ready_key(repo, pr.number, pr.head_sha),
        actor=pr.author or actor_of(payload, "github"),
    )


def ready_trigger_from_gitlab(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> ReviewTrigger | None:
    if payload.get("object_kind") != "merge_request":
        return None
    attrs = payload.get("object_attributes")
    project = _map(payload.get("project"))
    repo = _str(project.get("path_with_namespace"))
    if not isinstance(attrs, Mapping) or not repo:
        return None
    changes = _map(payload.get("changes"))
    became_ready = any(
        _map(changes.get(key)).get("previous") is True and _map(changes.get(key)).get("current") is False
        for key in _DRAFT_KEYS
    )
    opened_ready = _str(attrs.get("action")) == "open" and not _gitlab_draft(attrs)
    if not (became_ready or opened_ready):
        return None
    pr = pr_from_gitlab(attrs, repo=repo)
    if pr is None or not pr.is_open:
        return None
    # The hook names the author only by id; `user` is who opened it or
    # marked it ready.
    return ReviewTrigger(
        pr=pr,
        trigger="ready",
        trigger_key=ready_key(repo, pr.number, pr.head_sha),
        actor=actor_of(payload, "gitlab") or pr.author,
    )


def command_from_github(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> ReviewCommand | None:
    if payload.get("action") != "created":
        return None
    issue = _map(payload.get("issue"))
    comment = _map(payload.get("comment"))
    if not issue.get("pull_request") or not has_review_command(_str(comment.get("body"))):
        return None
    repository = _map(payload.get("repository"))
    repo = _str(repository.get("full_name"))
    number = _int(issue.get("number"))
    comment_id = comment.get("id")
    if not repo or number is None or comment_id is None:
        return None
    user = _map(comment.get("user"))
    return ReviewCommand(
        forge="github",
        repo=repo,
        number=number,
        comment_id=str(comment_id),
        author=_str(user.get("login")),
        github_comment=comment,
    )


def command_from_gitlab(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> ReviewCommand | None:
    if payload.get("object_kind") != "note":
        return None
    attrs = _map(payload.get("object_attributes"))
    if attrs.get("noteable_type") != "MergeRequest" or attrs.get("system") is True:
        return None
    if not has_review_command(_str(attrs.get("note"))):
        return None
    project = _map(payload.get("project"))
    mr = _map(payload.get("merge_request"))
    repo = _str(project.get("path_with_namespace"))
    number = _int(mr.get("iid"))
    note_id = attrs.get("id")
    if not repo or number is None or note_id is None:
        return None
    user = _map(payload.get("user"))
    return ReviewCommand(
        forge="gitlab",
        repo=repo,
        number=number,
        comment_id=str(note_id),
        author=_str(user.get("username")),
        gitlab_user_id=_int(user.get("id")),
        gitlab_mr=mr,
    )
```

  tests/fixtures/webhooks/payloads.py tests/fixtures/webhooks/__init__.py \
  tests/unit/services/pr_review/__init__.py tests/unit/services/pr_review/test_triggers.py
git commit -F - <<'MSG'
feat(pr-review): parse review triggers from forge payloads

A review starts when a pull request becomes ready or when someone
comments /embry0 review. Both forges spell these differently, and
older GitLab names the draft flag work_in_progress, so one pure parser
turns each payload into a trigger or nothing.

The command is a line prefix outside code fences and quotes, and a
comment carrying embry0's marker never counts, so embry0 cannot ask
itself for a review. A missing head repository counts as a fork.

Refs: #30
Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_01Q5pSvJSg6T12CagYZMAHYy
MSG

- Consumes: Task 1 `PrReviewsRepository` (`exists`, `claim`, `attach_job`, `release`); Task 2 parsers and dataclasses; `ReposRepository.registered_as(repo) -> str | None`, `ReposRepository.forge_for(repo) -> str`; `RepoPreferencesRepository.get(repo) -> dict | None`; `JobsRepository.find_by_pr_url(url) -> list[dict]`; `IssueExecutor.dispatch_refusal(*, actor) -> DispatchDecision | None` (exists); `GitHubClient.get_pull(repo, number) -> dict | None` (exists); `check_comment_author`, `parse_author_roles`, `DEFAULT_AUTHOR_ROLES`.
- Produces:
  - `embry0.services.pr_review.settings`: `DEPTHS = ("full", "targeted", "read_only")`; `@dataclass(frozen=True) ReviewSettings(enabled: bool = False, depth: str = "full", model: str | None = None)`; `review_settings(prefs: Mapping[str, Any] | None) -> ReviewSettings`; `effective_depth(settings: ReviewSettings, *, from_fork: bool) -> str`.
  - `embry0.services.pr_review.dispatcher`: `@dataclass(frozen=True) DispatchResult(dispatched: bool, reason: str, job_id: str | None = None)` with `as_dict() -> dict[str, Any]`; `build_review_config(trigger, *, repo, settings, review_id) -> dict[str, Any]` (the job's `pipeline_config`, keys: `pipeline, review_id, trigger_key, repo, forge, number, url, title, body, author, head_ref, head_sha, base_ref, from_fork, trigger, depth, model`); `class PrReviewDispatcher(*, config, repos_repo, prefs_repo, jobs_repo, reviews_repo, executor, github=None, gitlab=None)` with `async handle_github_pull_request(payload, action) -> DispatchResult | None`, `async handle_github_comment(payload) -> DispatchResult | None`, `async handle_gitlab_merge_request(payload) -> DispatchResult | None`, `async handle_gitlab_note(payload) -> DispatchResult | None`, `async dispatch_manual(*, repo: str, number: int, actor: str) -> DispatchResult`. `None` means "this delivery is not a review trigger".
  - The executor contract the dispatcher calls (implemented in Task 10): `async start_pr_review_job(*, repo: str, review: dict[str, Any], dispatched_by: str) -> str` (returns the job id).
  - `GitLabClient.get_merge_request(repo: str, iid: int) -> dict[str, Any] | None`; `GitLabClient.get_member_access_level(repo: str, user_id: int) -> int | None`.
  - `dispatch_actors.PR_REVIEW = "embry0:pr-review"`.

```python
"""GitLabClient calls the pull request reviewer needs (#30)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json

import httpx
import pytest

from embry0.execution.gitlab_client import GitLabClient


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _clean_namespace_tokens(monkeypatch):
    import os

    for key in [k for k in os.environ if k.startswith("GITLAB_TOKEN__")]:
        monkeypatch.delenv(key)


def _client(handler) -> GitLabClient:
    async def _sleep(_seconds: float) -> None:
        return None

    http = httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
    return GitLabClient(
        default_token="glpat-abc", gitlab_url="https://gitlab.example.com", http_client=http, sleep=_sleep
    )


async def test_member_access_level_reads_the_inherited_membership() -> None:
    seen: list[str] = []

    def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        seen.append(request.url.raw_path.decode())
        return httpx.Response(200, json={"id": 42, "access_level": 30})

    level = await _client(handler).get_member_access_level("acme/products/web", 42)

    assert level == 30
    assert seen == ["/api/v4/projects/acme%2Fproducts%2Fweb/members/all/42"], "inherited members live under /all"


async def test_a_non_member_has_no_access_level() -> None:
    client = _client(lambda _r: httpx.Response(404, json={"message": "404 Not found"}))
    assert await client.get_member_access_level("acme/products/web", 42) is None


async def test_get_merge_request_returns_the_record_or_none() -> None:
    def handler(request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        if request.url.raw_path.decode().endswith("/merge_requests/5"):
            return httpx.Response(200, content=json.dumps({"iid": 5, "sha": "c" * 40}))
        return httpx.Response(404)

    client = _client(handler)
    assert (await client.get_merge_request("acme/products/web", 5)) == {"iid": 5, "sha": "c" * 40}
    assert await client.get_merge_request("acme/products/web", 6) is None
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the two read calls**

In `embry0/execution/gitlab_client.py`, add to `GitLabClient` after `get_issue`:

```python
    async def get_merge_request(self, repo: str, iid: int) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        """One merge request by its project-scoped ``iid``, or None when missing or unreadable."""
        resp = await self.get(self.project_path(repo, f"/merge_requests/{iid}"), repo=repo)
        if resp.status_code != 200:
            return None
        body = resp.json()
        return body if isinstance(body, dict) else None

    async def get_member_access_level(self, repo: str, user_id: int) -> int | None:
        """The user's access level on the project, inherited memberships included.

        ``/members/all`` counts group membership too; plain ``/members`` would
        refuse a Developer who is one only through the group. None when the
        user is not a member or the lookup failed.
        """
        resp = await self.get(self.project_path(repo, f"/members/all/{user_id}"), repo=repo)
        if resp.status_code != 200:
            return None
        body = resp.json()
        level = body.get("access_level") if isinstance(body, dict) else None
        return level if isinstance(level, int) and not isinstance(level, bool) else None
```

"""Reading repo_preferences.pr_review (#30)."""

import pytest

from embry0.services.pr_review.settings import ReviewSettings, effective_depth, review_settings


@pytest.mark.parametrize("prefs", [None, {}, {"pr_review": None}, {"pr_review": "on"}])
def test_an_unconfigured_repo_is_off_full_and_on_the_heavy_tier(prefs) -> None:
    assert review_settings(prefs) == ReviewSettings(enabled=False, depth="full", model=None)


def test_stored_settings_are_read() -> None:
    prefs = {"pr_review": {"enabled": True, "depth": "targeted", "model": "claude-sonnet-5"}}
    assert review_settings(prefs) == ReviewSettings(enabled=True, depth="targeted", model="claude-sonnet-5")


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("raw", "expected"),
    [
        ({"enabled": "true"}, ReviewSettings()),  # only a real true turns it on
        ({"enabled": True, "depth": "deep"}, ReviewSettings(enabled=True)),
        ({"enabled": True, "model": "  "}, ReviewSettings(enabled=True)),
    ],
)
def test_odd_stored_values_fall_back_to_the_defaults(raw, expected) -> None:
    assert review_settings({"pr_review": raw}) == expected


def test_a_fork_is_always_read_only() -> None:
    settings = ReviewSettings(enabled=True, depth="full")
    assert effective_depth(settings, from_fork=True) == "read_only"
    assert effective_depth(settings, from_fork=False) == "full"
```

Create `tests/unit/services/pr_review/test_dispatcher.py`:

```python
"""PrReviewDispatcher — the five gates, dedupe and job creation (#30)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from types import SimpleNamespace
from typing import Any

import pytest

from embry0.orchestration import dispatch_actors
from embry0.orchestration.budget_gate import DispatchDecision
from embry0.services.pr_review.dispatcher import PrReviewDispatcher
from tests.fixtures.webhooks import (
    github_issue_comment,
    github_review_pull_request,
    gitlab_merge_request,
    gitlab_mr_note,
)

SHA = "a" * 40
ENABLED = {"pr_review": {"enabled": True, "depth": "full", "model": None}}


class _Repos:
    def __init__(self, registered: set[str]) -> None:
        self.registered = registered

    async def registered_as(self, repo: str) -> str | None:
        return repo if repo in self.registered else None

    async def forge_for(self, repo: str) -> str:
        return "gitlab" if repo.startswith("acme/products/") else "github"


class _Prefs:
    def __init__(self, rows: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
        self.rows = rows

    async def get(self, repo: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        return self.rows.get(repo)


class _Jobs:
    def __init__(self, by_url: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] | None = None) -> None:
        self.by_url = by_url or {}

    async def find_by_pr_url(self, pr_url: str, limit: int = 5) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.by_url.get(pr_url, [])


class _Reviews:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.rows: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}

    async def exists(self, trigger_key: str) -> bool:
        return any(r["trigger_key"] == trigger_key for r in self.rows.values())

    async def claim(self, *, repo: str, number: int, head_sha: str, trigger: str, trigger_key: str) -> str | None:
        if await self.exists(trigger_key):
            return None
        review_id = f"prr-{len(self.rows) + 1}"
        self.rows[review_id] = {
            "repo": repo, "number": number, "head_sha": head_sha, "trigger": trigger, "trigger_key": trigger_key,
            "job_id": None,
        }
        return review_id

    async def attach_job(self, review_id: str, job_id: str) -> None:
        self.rows[review_id]["job_id"] = job_id

    async def release(self, review_id: str) -> None:
        self.rows.pop(review_id, None)


class _Executor:
    def __init__(self, *, refusal: DispatchDecision | None = None, fail: bool = False) -> None:
        self.refusal = refusal
        self.fail = fail
        self.started: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.asked_actor: list[str | None] = []

    async def dispatch_refusal(self, *, actor: str | None = None) -> DispatchDecision | None:
        self.asked_actor.append(actor)
        return self.refusal

    async def start_pr_review_job(self, *, repo: str, review: dict[str, Any], dispatched_by: str) -> str:
        if self.fail:
            raise RuntimeError("database is down")
        self.started.append({"repo": repo, "review": review, "dispatched_by": dispatched_by})
        return f"job-{len(self.started)}"


class _DbExecutor(_Executor):
    """Creates real job rows, for the test that runs against Postgres (pr_reviews.job_id is a foreign key)."""

    def __init__(self, db: Any) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.db = db

    async def start_pr_review_job(self, *, repo: str, review: dict[str, Any], dispatched_by: str) -> str:
        from embry0.storage.repositories.jobs import JobsRepository

        self.started.append({"repo": repo, "review": review, "dispatched_by": dispatched_by})
        return await JobsRepository(self.db).create(
            repo=repo, task="t", pipeline_template="pr-review", pipeline_config=review, dispatched_by=dispatched_by
        )


class _GitHub:
    def __init__(self, pull: dict[str, Any] | None) -> None:
        self.pull = pull

    async def get_pull(self, repo: str, number: int) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        return self.pull


class _GitLab:
    def __init__(self, level: int | None) -> None:
        self.level = level
        self.asked: list[tuple[str, int]] = []

    async def get_member_access_level(self, repo: str, user_id: int) -> int | None:
        self.asked.append((repo, user_id))
        return self.level

    async def get_merge_request(self, repo: str, iid: int) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        return gitlab_merge_request(iid=iid, project_path=repo)["object_attributes"]


def _dispatcher(
    *,
    prefs: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    registered: set[str] | None = None,
    jobs: _Jobs | None = None,
    executor: _Executor | None = None,
    github: _GitHub | None = None,
    gitlab: _GitLab | None = None,
    reviews: Any = None,
) -> tuple[PrReviewDispatcher, _Executor, Any]:
    executor = executor or _Executor()
    reviews = reviews if reviews is not None else _Reviews()
    dispatcher = PrReviewDispatcher(
        config=SimpleNamespace(comment_dispatch_author_roles="OWNER,MEMBER,COLLABORATOR"),
        repos_repo=_Repos(registered if registered is not None else {"acme/widgets", "acme/products/web"}),
        prefs_repo=_Prefs(prefs if prefs is not None else {"acme/widgets": ENABLED, "acme/products/web": ENABLED}),
        jobs_repo=jobs or _Jobs(),
        reviews_repo=reviews,
        executor=executor,
        github=github,
        gitlab=gitlab,
    )
    return dispatcher, executor, reviews


# ---- not a trigger ---------------------------------------------------------


async def test_a_push_is_not_a_review_trigger() -> None:
    dispatcher, executor, _ = _dispatcher()
    assert await dispatcher.handle_github_pull_request(github_review_pull_request(action="synchronize"), "synchronize") is None
    assert executor.started == []


async def test_a_tag_only_comment_is_not_a_review_trigger() -> None:
    dispatcher, _, _ = _dispatcher()
    payload = github_issue_comment(body="@embry0 fix it", repo="acme/widgets", number=7, on_pull_request=True)
    assert await dispatcher.handle_github_comment(payload) is None


# ---- gate 1 ----------------------------------------------------------------


async def test_a_disabled_repo_is_refused() -> None:
    dispatcher, executor, _ = _dispatcher(prefs={})
    result = await dispatcher.handle_github_pull_request(github_review_pull_request(), "ready_for_review")
    assert result is not None and (result.dispatched, result.reason) == (False, "review_disabled")
    assert executor.started == [] and executor.asked_actor == [], "a refused trigger must not reach the budget gate"


async def test_an_unregistered_repo_is_refused() -> None:
    dispatcher, executor, _ = _dispatcher(registered=set())
    result = await dispatcher.handle_github_pull_request(github_review_pull_request(), "ready_for_review")
    assert result is not None and result.reason == "repo_not_registered"
    assert executor.started == []


# ---- gate 2 ----------------------------------------------------------------


async def test_embry0s_own_branch_is_refused() -> None:
    dispatcher, executor, _ = _dispatcher()
    payload = github_review_pull_request(head_ref="embry0/iss-abcd1234-fix-login")
    result = await dispatcher.handle_github_pull_request(payload, "ready_for_review")
    assert result is not None and result.reason == "own_pull_request"
    assert executor.started == []


async def test_a_pull_request_a_job_opened_is_refused() -> None:
    url = "https://github.com/acme/widgets/pull/7"
    jobs = _Jobs({url: [{"job_id": "job-old", "pipeline_template": None}]})
    dispatcher, executor, _ = _dispatcher(jobs=jobs)
    result = await dispatcher.handle_github_pull_request(github_review_pull_request(), "ready_for_review")
    assert result is not None and result.reason == "own_pull_request"


async def test_a_follow_up_or_an_earlier_review_does_not_make_it_ours() -> None:
    url = "https://github.com/acme/widgets/pull/7"
    jobs = _Jobs({url: [{"job_id": "job-f", "pipeline_template": "pr-followup"}, {"job_id": "job-r", "pipeline_template": "pr-review"}]})
    dispatcher, executor, _ = _dispatcher(jobs=jobs)
    result = await dispatcher.handle_github_pull_request(github_review_pull_request(), "ready_for_review")
    assert result is not None and result.dispatched, f"refused as {result and result.reason}"


# ---- gate 3 ----------------------------------------------------------------


def _pull(**overrides: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {**github_review_pull_request()["pull_request"], **overrides}


async def test_a_trusted_github_comment_starts_a_command_review() -> None:
    dispatcher, executor, reviews = _dispatcher(github=_GitHub(_pull()))
    payload = github_issue_comment(body="/embry0 review", repo="acme/widgets", number=7, on_pull_request=True)
    result = await dispatcher.handle_github_comment(payload)
    assert result is not None and result.dispatched and result.job_id == "job-1"
    review = executor.started[0]["review"]
    assert (review["trigger"], review["trigger_key"], review["head_sha"]) == ("command", "command:github:1001", SHA)
    assert executor.started[0]["dispatched_by"] == "octocat" and executor.asked_actor == ["octocat"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("association", "login", "user_type", "reason"),
    [
        ("CONTRIBUTOR", "octocat", "User", "author_association_CONTRIBUTOR_not_allowed"),
        ("NONE", "octocat", "User", "author_association_NONE_not_allowed"),
        ("OWNER", "ci[bot]", "User", "bot_author"),
        ("OWNER", "app", "Bot", "bot_author"),
    ],
)
async def test_an_untrusted_github_comment_is_refused(association: str, login: str, user_type: str, reason: str) -> None:
    dispatcher, executor, _ = _dispatcher(github=_GitHub(_pull()))
    payload = github_issue_comment(body="/embry0 review", repo="acme/widgets", number=7, login=login, association=association, on_pull_request=True)
    payload["comment"]["user"]["type"] = user_type
    result = await dispatcher.handle_github_comment(payload)
    assert result is not None and (result.dispatched, result.reason) == (False, reason)
    assert executor.started == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("level", "dispatched"), [(20, False), (None, False), (30, True), (40, True)])
async def test_gitlab_commenters_need_developer_access(level: int | None, dispatched: bool) -> None:
    gitlab = _GitLab(level)
    dispatcher, executor, _ = _dispatcher(gitlab=gitlab)
    result = await dispatcher.handle_gitlab_note(gitlab_mr_note())
    assert result is not None and result.dispatched is dispatched, f"access level {level}: {result and result.reason}"
    assert gitlab.asked == [("acme/products/web", 42)]
    if not dispatched:
        assert result.reason.startswith("access_level_")


async def test_a_command_on_a_closed_pull_request_is_refused() -> None:
    dispatcher, executor, _ = _dispatcher(github=_GitHub(_pull(state="closed")))
    payload = github_issue_comment(body="/embry0 review", repo="acme/widgets", number=7, on_pull_request=True)
    result = await dispatcher.handle_github_comment(payload)
    assert result is not None and result.reason == "pr_not_open"


async def test_a_command_whose_pull_request_cannot_be_read_is_refused() -> None:
    dispatcher, _, _ = _dispatcher(github=_GitHub(None))
    payload = github_issue_comment(body="/embry0 review", repo="acme/widgets", number=7, on_pull_request=True)
    result = await dispatcher.handle_github_comment(payload)
    assert result is not None and result.reason == "pr_unreadable"


# ---- gate 4 ----------------------------------------------------------------


async def test_a_second_delivery_of_the_same_ready_event_is_refused() -> None:
    dispatcher, executor, _ = _dispatcher()
    payload = github_review_pull_request()
    first = await dispatcher.handle_github_pull_request(payload, "ready_for_review")
    second = await dispatcher.handle_github_pull_request(payload, "ready_for_review")
    assert first is not None and first.dispatched
    assert second is not None and (second.dispatched, second.reason) == (False, "duplicate")
    assert len(executor.started) == 1


async def test_a_new_head_is_a_new_ready_review() -> None:
    dispatcher, executor, _ = _dispatcher()
    await dispatcher.handle_github_pull_request(github_review_pull_request(), "ready_for_review")
    again = await dispatcher.handle_github_pull_request(github_review_pull_request(head_sha="b" * 40), "ready_for_review")
    assert again is not None and again.dispatched


# ---- gate 5 ----------------------------------------------------------------


async def test_over_budget_is_refused_before_anything_is_claimed() -> None:
    refusal = DispatchDecision(allowed=False, limit="daily", message="capped")
    dispatcher, executor, reviews = _dispatcher(executor=_Executor(refusal=refusal))
    result = await dispatcher.handle_github_pull_request(github_review_pull_request(), "ready_for_review")
    assert result is not None and (result.dispatched, result.reason) == (False, "budget_daily")
    assert reviews.rows == {}, "a refused review must not hold its key, or a later redelivery could never run it"
    assert executor.started == []


async def test_a_ready_event_charges_the_pull_requests_author() -> None:
    dispatcher, executor, _ = _dispatcher()
    await dispatcher.handle_github_pull_request(github_review_pull_request(author="mona"), "ready_for_review")
    assert executor.asked_actor == ["mona"] and executor.started[0]["dispatched_by"] == "mona"


async def test_a_nameless_trigger_is_charged_to_the_service_actor() -> None:
    dispatcher, executor, _ = _dispatcher()
    payload = github_review_pull_request(author="")
    payload["sender"] = {}
    await dispatcher.handle_github_pull_request(payload, "ready_for_review")
    assert executor.started[0]["dispatched_by"] == dispatch_actors.PR_REVIEW

async def test_the_job_carries_the_whole_review_config() -> None:
    prefs = {"acme/widgets": {"pr_review": {"enabled": True, "depth": "targeted", "model": "claude-sonnet-5"}}}
    dispatcher, executor, reviews = _dispatcher(prefs=prefs)
    result = await dispatcher.handle_github_pull_request(github_review_pull_request(), "ready_for_review")

    review = executor.started[0]["review"]

        "pipeline": "pr-review",
        "review_id": "prr-1",
        "trigger_key": f"ready:acme/widgets#7@{SHA}",
        "repo": "acme/widgets",
        "forge": "github",
        "number": 7,
        "url": "https://github.com/acme/widgets/pull/7",
        "title": "Add the login form",
        "body": "Adds the login form and its validation.",
        "author": "octocat",
        "head_ref": "feature/login",
        "head_sha": SHA,
        "base_ref": "main",
        "from_fork": False,
        "trigger": "ready",
        "depth": "targeted",
        "model": "claude-sonnet-5",
    }
    assert reviews.rows["prr-1"]["job_id"] == "job-1"


async def test_a_fork_is_reviewed_read_only_whatever_the_depth() -> None:

    review = executor.started[0]["review"]

async def test_a_gitlab_fork_is_reviewed_read_only() -> None:

    await dispatcher.handle_gitlab_merge_request(gitlab_merge_request(source_project_id=9))
    review = executor.started[0]["review"]
    assert (review["forge"], review["from_fork"], review["depth"]) == ("gitlab", True, "read_only")


async def test_a_job_that_cannot_be_created_gives_its_claim_back() -> None:
    dispatcher, executor, reviews = _dispatcher(executor=_Executor(fail=True))
    result = await dispatcher.handle_github_pull_request(github_review_pull_request(), "ready_for_review")
    assert result is not None and (result.dispatched, result.reason) == (False, "dispatch_failed")
    assert reviews.rows == {}, "the claim must be released so a redelivery can try again"


# ---- manual ----------------------------------------------------------------


async def test_a_manual_review_skips_the_enabled_setting() -> None:

async def test_a_manual_review_still_refuses_an_unregistered_repo() -> None:

    result = await dispatcher.dispatch_manual(repo="acme/widgets", number=7, actor="ops@example.com")
    assert (result.dispatched, result.reason) == (False, "repo_not_registered")


async def test_a_manual_gitlab_review_reads_the_merge_request() -> None:
    dispatcher, executor, _ = _dispatcher(gitlab=_GitLab(40))
    result = await dispatcher.dispatch_manual(repo="acme/products/web", number=5, actor="ops@example.com")
    assert result.dispatched, result.reason
    assert executor.started[0]["review"]["forge"] == "gitlab"


# ---- dedupe against Postgres -----------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.requires_postgres
async def test_a_redelivery_is_refused_by_the_unique_key(db_with_migrations) -> None:
    from embry0.storage.repositories.pr_reviews import PrReviewsRepository

    reviews = PrReviewsRepository(db_with_migrations)
    dispatcher, executor, _ = _dispatcher(reviews=reviews, executor=_DbExecutor(db_with_migrations))
    payload = github_review_pull_request()

    first = await dispatcher.handle_github_pull_request(payload, "ready_for_review")
    second = await dispatcher.handle_github_pull_request(payload, "ready_for_review")

    assert first is not None and first.dispatched
    assert second is not None and second.reason == "duplicate"
    count = await db_with_migrations.fetchval("SELECT count(*) FROM pr_reviews")
    assert count == 1, f"{count} pr_reviews rows for one ready event"
