```python
"""The executor runs pr-review jobs (#30)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import inspect
from types import SimpleNamespace
from typing import Any
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock, patch

from embry0.orchestration.budget_gate import DispatchDecision
from embry0.orchestration.restart import NOT_RESUMABLE_PIPELINES
from embry0.safety.error_codes import ErrorCode
from embry0.services._executor_run import _READ_ONLY_PIPELINES
from embry0.services.issue_executor import IssueExecutor
from embry0.storage.repositories.pr_reviews import PrReviewsRepository
from embry0.workflows.pr_review.graph import PrReviewWorkflow

REVIEW = {
    "pipeline": "pr-review",
    "review_id": "prr-1",
    "repo": "acme/widgets",
    "forge": "github",
    "number": 7,
    "title": "Add the login form",
    "head_sha": "a" * 40,
    "from_fork": False,
    "trigger": "ready",
    "depth": "full",
    "model": None,
}


class _Jobs:
    def __init__(self, row: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> None:
        self.row = row
        self.updates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.created: dict[str, Any] = {}
        self.launch: dict[str, Any] | None = None

    async def get(self, job_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        return self.row

    async def update(self, job_id: str, **fields: Any) -> None:
        self.updates.append(fields)

    async def create(self, **kwargs: Any) -> str:
        self.created = kwargs
        return "job-1"

    async def record_launch(self, job_id: str, launch: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        self.launch = launch


def _executor(jobs: _Jobs) -> IssueExecutor:
    executor = IssueExecutor(
        issues_repo=MagicMock(),
        jobs_repo=jobs,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        traces_repo=MagicMock(),
        workflow_registry=MagicMock(),
        database_url="postgresql://unused/unused",
    )
    executor._audit_log_path = None
    executor._budget_gate = SimpleNamespace(check=AsyncMock(return_value=DispatchDecision(allowed=True)))
    executor._finish_credential_uses = AsyncMock()  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    executor._cleanup_sandbox = AsyncMock()  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    executor._load_merged_env_vars = AsyncMock(return_value=[{"key": "A", "value": "1", "scope": "app"}])  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    return executor


def _row(**review: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"job_id": "job-1", "repo": "acme/widgets", "task": "Review", "dispatched_by": "octocat", "pipeline_config": {**REVIEW, **review}}


def test_the_pipeline_is_selected_and_never_resumed_or_given_push() -> None:
    assert isinstance(_executor(_Jobs())._select_workflow("pr-review"), PrReviewWorkflow)
    assert "pr-review" in NOT_RESUMABLE_PIPELINES, "a review fans out over a sandbox a resume would not re-enroll"
    assert "pr-review" in _READ_ONLY_PIPELINES, "a review sandbox must never be enrolled with git_push"


def test_the_dispatcher_and_the_executor_agree_on_the_contract() -> None:
    params = inspect.signature(IssueExecutor.start_pr_review_job).parameters
    assert {n: p.kind for n, p in params.items() if n != "self"} == {
        "repo": inspect.Parameter.KEYWORD_ONLY,
        "review": inspect.Parameter.KEYWORD_ONLY,
        "dispatched_by": inspect.Parameter.KEYWORD_ONLY,
    }


async def test_start_creates_the_job_and_records_how_to_launch_it_again() -> None:
    jobs = _Jobs()
    executor = _executor(jobs)
    run = AsyncMock()
    with patch.object(executor, "_run_pr_review_workflow", run), patch("embry0.services.issue_executor.emit_audit", new=AsyncMock()):
        job_id = await executor.start_pr_review_job(repo="acme/widgets", review=REVIEW, dispatched_by="octocat")
        await executor._tasks_by_job[job_id]

    assert job_id == "job-1"
    assert jobs.created == {
        "repo": "acme/widgets",
        "task": "Review acme/widgets#7: Add the login form",
        "pipeline_template": "pr-review",
        "pipeline_config": REVIEW,
        "sandbox_profile": None,
        "trace_id": jobs.created["trace_id"],
        "dispatched_by": "octocat",
    }
    assert "pr_url" not in jobs.created, "a review job must not claim the pull request as its own"
    assert jobs.launch == {"runner": "pr-review"}
    run.assert_awaited_once_with("job-1")


async def test_a_relaunch_runs_the_review_again() -> None:
    executor = _executor(_Jobs())
    run = AsyncMock()
    with patch.object(executor, "_run_pr_review_workflow", run):
        await executor._relaunch("job-1", {"runner": "pr-review"})
    run.assert_awaited_once_with("job-1")


def _stream(final_state: dict[str, Any] | None, seen: dict[str, Any]) -> AsyncMock:
    async def run(initial_state: dict[str, Any], **kwargs: Any) -> tuple[dict[str, Any] | None, bool, None]:
        seen.update(initial_state=initial_state, **kwargs)
        sink = kwargs.get("state_sink")
        if sink is not None:
            sink["sandbox_container_id"] = "sandbox-1"
        return final_state, False, None

    return AsyncMock(side_effect=run)


async def test_a_published_review_completes_the_job_with_its_summary() -> None:
    jobs = _Jobs(_row())
    executor = _executor(jobs)
    seen: dict[str, Any] = {}
    final = {"pr_review": {"final_status": "completed", "verdict": {"summary": "Looks careful."}}}
    with patch.object(executor, "_execute_workflow_stream", _stream(final, seen)):
        await executor._run_pr_review_workflow("job-1")

    assert jobs.updates[0]["status"] == "running"
    assert (jobs.updates[-1]["status"], jobs.updates[-1]["result_summary"]) == ("completed", "Looks careful.")
    assert seen["pipeline"] == "pr-review"
    assert seen["initial_state"]["pr_review"] == REVIEW
    assert seen["initial_state"]["agent_models_override"] == {"pr-reviewer": "claude-opus-5"}, "no override means the heavy tier"
    assert seen["initial_state"]["user_env_vars"] == [{"key": "A", "value": "1", "scope": "app"}]
    executor._cleanup_sandbox.assert_awaited_once_with({"sandbox_container_id": "sandbox-1"}, "job-1")


async def test_the_repos_model_override_wins() -> None:
    executor = _executor(_Jobs(_row(model="claude-sonnet-5")))
    seen: dict[str, Any] = {}
    with patch.object(executor, "_execute_workflow_stream", _stream({"pr_review": {"final_status": "completed"}}, seen)):
        await executor._run_pr_review_workflow("job-1")
    assert seen["initial_state"]["agent_models_override"] == {"pr-reviewer": "claude-sonnet-5"}


async def test_a_fork_gets_no_env_vars() -> None:
    executor = _executor(_Jobs(_row(from_fork=True, depth="read_only")))
    seen: dict[str, Any] = {}
    with patch.object(executor, "_execute_workflow_stream", _stream({"pr_review": {"final_status": "completed"}}, seen)):
        await executor._run_pr_review_workflow("job-1")
    assert "user_env_vars" not in seen["initial_state"]
    executor._load_merged_env_vars.assert_not_awaited()


async def test_a_failed_review_fails_the_job_with_its_own_code() -> None:
    jobs = _Jobs(_row())
    executor = _executor(jobs)
    final = {"pr_review": {"final_status": "failed", "error_code": ErrorCode.PR_REVIEW_PUBLISH_FAILED.value, "failure_summary": "GitHub refused the review (403)"}}
    with patch.object(executor, "_execute_workflow_stream", _stream(final, {})):
        await executor._run_pr_review_workflow("job-1")
    last = jobs.updates[-1]
    assert (last["status"], last["error_code"], last["error_message"]) == (
        "failed",
        ErrorCode.PR_REVIEW_PUBLISH_FAILED.value,
        "GitHub refused the review (403)",
    )
    executor._finish_credential_uses.assert_awaited_with("job-1", error_code=ErrorCode.PR_REVIEW_PUBLISH_FAILED.value)


async def test_a_crash_fails_the_job_and_still_destroys_the_sandbox() -> None:
    jobs = _Jobs(_row())
    executor = _executor(jobs)
    with patch.object(executor, "_execute_workflow_stream", AsyncMock(side_effect=RuntimeError("graph exploded"))):
        await executor._run_pr_review_workflow("job-1")
    assert jobs.updates[-1]["status"] == "failed" and "graph exploded" in jobs.updates[-1]["error_message"]
    executor._cleanup_sandbox.assert_awaited_once()


def test_the_graph_config_carries_the_reviews_repository() -> None:
    executor = IssueExecutor(
        issues_repo=MagicMock(),
        jobs_repo=MagicMock(),
        traces_repo=MagicMock(),
        workflow_registry=MagicMock(),
        database_url="postgresql://unused/unused",
        db=MagicMock(),
    )
    assert isinstance(executor._build_graph_config("job-1")["configurable"]["pr_reviews_repo"], PrReviewsRepository)
```

```python
"""POST /api/v1/jobs {pipeline: "pr-review"} — the manual review trigger (#30)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from typing import Any
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, patch

import pytest
from httpx import ASGITransport, AsyncClient

from embry0.api.app import create_app
from embry0.config import Embry0Config
from embry0.services.pr_review.dispatcher import DispatchResult
from embry0.storage.repositories.repos import UnregisteredRepoError


class _Dispatcher:
    def __init__(self, result: DispatchResult | Exception) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    async def dispatch_manual(self, *, repo: str, number: int, actor: str) -> DispatchResult:
        self.calls.append({"repo": repo, "number": number, "actor": actor})
        if isinstance(self.result, Exception):
            raise self.result
        return self.result


class _Jobs:
    async def get(self, job_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"job_id": job_id, "pipeline_template": "pr-review"}


def _app(dispatcher: _Dispatcher | None):
    @asynccontextmanager
    async def _noop(_app):
        yield

    config = Embry0Config(_env_file=None, auth_dev_mode=True, webhook_dev_mode=True)
    app = create_app(config, lifespan_override=_noop)
    app.state.config = config
    app.state.jobs_repo = _Jobs()
    if dispatcher is not None:
        app.state.pr_review_dispatcher = dispatcher
    return app


async def _post(app, body: dict[str, Any]):
    async with AsyncClient(transport=ASGITransport(app=app), base_url="http://test") as client:
        with patch("embry0.api.v1.jobs.emit_audit", new=AsyncMock()):
            return await client.post("/api/v1/jobs", json=body, headers={"X-Requested-With": "XMLHttpRequest"})


async def test_a_manual_review_is_dispatched_and_returns_the_job() -> None:
    dispatcher = _Dispatcher(DispatchResult(True, "ok", "job-9"))
    resp = await _post(_app(dispatcher), {"pipeline": "pr-review", "repo": "acme/widgets", "number": 7})
    assert resp.status_code == 201, resp.text
    assert resp.json() == {"job_id": "job-9", "pipeline_template": "pr-review"}
    assert dispatcher.calls[0]["repo"] == "acme/widgets" and dispatcher.calls[0]["number"] == 7


@pytest.mark.parametrize("body", [{"pipeline": "pr-review", "repo": "acme/widgets"}, {"pipeline": "pr-review", "number": 7}])
async def test_a_manual_review_needs_a_repo_and_a_number(body: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    resp = await _post(_app(_Dispatcher(DispatchResult(True, "ok", "job-9"))), body)
    assert resp.status_code == 422, resp.text


async def test_a_refused_review_says_why() -> None:
    resp = await _post(_app(_Dispatcher(DispatchResult(False, "own_pull_request"))), {"pipeline": "pr-review", "repo": "acme/widgets", "number": 7})
    assert resp.status_code == 409 and "own_pull_request" in resp.text


async def test_an_unregistered_repo_is_a_400() -> None:
    resp = await _post(_app(_Dispatcher(UnregisteredRepoError("acme/widgets"))), {"pipeline": "pr-review", "repo": "acme/widgets", "number": 7})
    assert resp.status_code == 400


async def test_without_a_dispatcher_it_is_a_503() -> None:
    resp = await _post(_app(None), {"pipeline": "pr-review", "repo": "acme/widgets", "number": 7})
    assert resp.status_code == 503
```

Create `tests/unit/api/test_pr_review_wiring.py`:

```python
"""Production wires the pull request reviewer (#30).

Read from the AST of app.py, as test_merge_approvals_wiring.py does: the
lifespan needs Docker and a database. Every collaborator below is optional
at construction, so a missing one would fail silently at the first review.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import ast
import pathlib

import pytest

import embry0.api.app as app_module

REQUIRED = ("config", "repos_repo", "prefs_repo", "jobs_repo", "reviews_repo", "executor", "github", "gitlab")


def _tree() -> ast.Module:
    return ast.parse(pathlib.Path(app_module.__file__).read_text())


def _call(name: str) -> ast.Call:
    for node in ast.walk(_tree()):
        if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and isinstance(node.func, ast.Name) and node.func.id == name:
            return node
    pytest.fail(f"embry0/api/app.py never calls {name}")


def test_the_dispatcher_gets_every_collaborator() -> None:
    passed = {kw.arg: kw.value for kw in _call("PrReviewDispatcher").keywords if kw.arg}
    missing = [name for name in REQUIRED if name not in passed]
    assert not missing, f"PrReviewDispatcher is built without {missing}"
    assert ast.unparse(passed["reviews_repo"]).startswith("PrReviewsRepository(")
    assert ast.unparse(passed["executor"]) == "app.state.issue_executor"


def test_the_dispatcher_is_where_the_webhooks_look_for_it() -> None:
    targets = {ast.unparse(t) for node in ast.walk(_tree()) if isinstance(node, ast.Assign) for t in node.targets}
    assert "app.state.pr_review_dispatcher" in targets


def test_the_reviewer_agent_is_seeded_at_startup() -> None:
    _call("seed_pr_reviewer_agent")
```

```python
"""`embry0 review <repo> <number>` is a thin client of POST /api/v1/jobs (#30)."""

from argparse import Namespace
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import pytest

from embry0.cli.__main__ import cmd_review


def _resp(status_code: int, payload: dict | None = None, text: str = "") -> MagicMock:
    resp = MagicMock()
    resp.status_code = status_code
    resp.json = MagicMock(return_value=payload or {})
    resp.text = text
    return resp


def test_it_posts_a_pr_review_job(monkeypatch, capsys) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setenv("EMBRY0_API_KEY", "sekrit")
    monkeypatch.delenv("EMBRY0_API_URL", raising=False)
    with patch("httpx.post", return_value=_resp(201, {"job_id": "job-9"})) as post:
        cmd_review(Namespace(repo="acme/widgets", number=7, api_url=None, api_key=None))
    args, kwargs = post.call_args
    assert args[0] == "http://localhost:8200/api/v1/jobs"
    assert kwargs["json"] == {"pipeline": "pr-review", "repo": "acme/widgets", "number": 7}
    assert kwargs["headers"]["Authorization"] == "Bearer sekrit"
    captured = capsys.readouterr()
    assert "job-9" in captured.out + captured.err


def test_a_refusal_exits_2_with_the_reason(monkeypatch, capsys) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setenv("EMBRY0_API_KEY", "k")
    with patch("httpx.post", return_value=_resp(409, text='{"detail":"review not started: duplicate"}')), pytest.raises(SystemExit) as exc:
        cmd_review(Namespace(repo="acme/widgets", number=7, api_url=None, api_key=None))
    assert exc.value.code == 2
    assert "duplicate" in capsys.readouterr().err
```

and add after `_run_onboard_workflow`:

```python
async def _heavy_tier_model(self: IssueExecutor) -> str:
    """The deployment's heavy tier (MODEL_HEAVY): the provider_config row, then the env, then the default."""
    from embry0.orchestration.nodes.agent import load_triage_model_settings

    _default, tiers = await load_triage_model_settings(
        {"configurable": {"db": getattr(self, "_db", None), "embry0_config": getattr(self, "_config", None)}}
    )
    return tiers["model_heavy"]


async def _run_pr_review_workflow(self: IssueExecutor, job_id: str) -> None:
    """Background runner for pr-review jobs (#30).

    Mirrors ``_run_onboard_workflow``: the graph's own nodes decide success
    (the review was posted) or failure (``pr_review.error_code``). Everything
    the run needs is the job row's ``pipeline_config``, so a relaunch after a
    restart needs no arguments. The sandbox is destroyed here too, from the
    last state the graph streamed, in case the graph raised before cleanup.
    """
    from datetime import UTC, datetime

    import structlog.contextvars as cv

    from embry0.workflows.pr_review.agent_seed import AGENT_TYPE

    trace_id_bound = False
    job_row: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    try:
        job_row = await self._jobs.get(job_id)
        trace_id = (job_row or {}).get("trace_id")
        if trace_id:
            cv.bind_contextvars(trace_id=trace_id)
            trace_id_bound = True
    except Exception:
        logger.warning("trace_id_bind_failed", job_id=job_id, exc_info=True)

    review = dict((job_row or {}).get("pipeline_config") or {})
    sink: dict[str, Any] = {}
    try:
        if await self._refuse_if_over_budget(job_id, actor=(job_row or {}).get("dispatched_by")):
            return
        await self._jobs.update(job_id, status="running", started_at=datetime.now(UTC))

        repo = str(review.get("repo") or (job_row or {}).get("repo") or "")
        initial_state: dict[str, Any] = {
            "job_id": job_id,
            "repo": repo,
            "task": str((job_row or {}).get("task") or f"Review {repo}"),
            "current_stage": "init",
            "agent_outputs": [],
            "errors": [],
            "retry_count": 0,
            "total_cost_usd": 0.0,
            "budget_overrun_usd": 0.0,
            "pr_review": review,
            # The model is explicit, so a deployment-wide default_model does
            # not move reviews off the heavy tier, and the LLM precheck sees
            # the provider the review will use.
            "agent_models_override": {AGENT_TYPE: str(review.get("model") or await _heavy_tier_model(self))},
        }
        if not review.get("from_fork") and review.get("depth") != "read_only":
            env_vars = await self._load_merged_env_vars(repo, job_id)
            if env_vars:
                initial_state["user_env_vars"] = env_vars

        final_state, interrupted, _ = await self._execute_workflow_stream(
            initial_state, issue_id="", job_id=job_id, pipeline="pr-review", state_sink=sink
        )
        if interrupted:
            logger.warning("pr_review_workflow_unexpected_interrupt", job_id=job_id)
            await self._jobs.update(
                job_id,
                status="failed",
                error_message="pr-review workflow interrupted unexpectedly",
                error_code=ErrorCode.UNEXPECTED_INTERRUPT.value,
                finished_at=datetime.now(UTC),
            )
            await self._finish_credential_uses(job_id, error_code=ErrorCode.UNEXPECTED_INTERRUPT.value)
            return

        rv = (final_state or {}).get("pr_review") or {}
        if rv.get("final_status") == "completed":
            summary = str((rv.get("verdict") or {}).get("summary") or "")
            await self._jobs.update(
                job_id, status="completed", finished_at=datetime.now(UTC), result_summary=summary[:4000] or None
            )
            await self._finish_credential_uses(job_id, error_code=None)
        else:
            code = str(rv.get("error_code") or ErrorCode.PR_REVIEW_NO_VERDICT.value)
            await self._jobs.update(
                job_id,
                status="failed",
                error_message=str(rv.get("failure_summary") or "the pull request review did not complete")[:4000],
                error_code=code,
                finished_at=datetime.now(UTC),
            )
            await self._finish_credential_uses(job_id, error_code=code)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.error("pr_review_workflow_crashed", job_id=job_id, error=str(exc), exc_info=True)
        crash_code = classify_failure(exc=exc, stage="pr-review").value
        try:
            await self._jobs.update(
                job_id,
                status="failed",
                error_message=f"pr-review workflow crashed: {exc}"[:4000],
                error_code=crash_code,
                finished_at=datetime.now(UTC),
            )
            await self._finish_credential_uses(job_id, error_code=crash_code)
        except StatusTransitionConflict:
            pass
    finally:
        await self._cleanup_sandbox(sink or None, job_id)
        if trace_id_bound:
            cv.unbind_contextvars("trace_id")
```

Note `_cleanup_sandbox` also runs for a budget-refused job (`sink` empty → `None`): it then only releases the job's volumes, which a refused job does not have.

```python
    async def start_pr_review_job(self, *, repo: str, review: dict[str, Any], dispatched_by: str) -> str:
        """#30: an issue-less pr-review job; ``review`` is its frozen ``pipeline_config``.

        Called by ``PrReviewDispatcher`` once every gate passed. The job never
        stores ``pr_url``: the pull request is not its own, and merge sync and
        the own-PR gate both read that column.
        """
        import uuid

        number = review.get("number")
        job_id = await self._jobs.create(
            repo=repo,
            task=f"Review {repo}#{number}: {review.get('title') or ''}".rstrip(": "),
            pipeline_template="pr-review",
            pipeline_config=dict(review),
            sandbox_profile=None,
            trace_id=f"trc-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}",
            dispatched_by=dispatched_by,
        )
        await emit_audit(
            self._db,
            "pr_review.job_created",
            actor="system",
            details={"job_id": job_id, "repo": repo, "number": number, "trigger": review.get("trigger")},
            audit_log_path=self._audit_log_path,
        )
        logger.info("pr_review_job_created", job_id=job_id, repo=repo, number=number, trigger=review.get("trigger"))
        self._track_task(
            self._run_pr_review_workflow(job_id),
            kind="pr_review_workflow_execute",
            job_id=job_id,
            launch={"runner": "pr-review"},
        )
        return job_id
```

In `embry0/api/v1/jobs.py`, add `from embry0.storage.repositories.repos import UnregisteredRepoError` to the imports and, after the onboard block and before the legacy issue-to-pr comment:

```python
    # #30: review one pull request now. Skips the repo's pr_review.enabled
    # setting, never its other gates; the dispatcher says why when it refuses.
    if req.pipeline == "pr-review":
        dispatcher = getattr(request.app.state, "pr_review_dispatcher", None)
        if dispatcher is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="pull request review is not initialized")
        assert req.repo is not None and req.number is not None  # noqa: S101 — schema-enforced
        try:
            result = await dispatcher.dispatch_manual(repo=req.repo, number=req.number, actor=request_actor(request))
        except UnregisteredRepoError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc
        if not result.dispatched or not result.job_id:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=f"review not started: {result.reason}")
        await emit_audit(
            getattr(request.app.state, "db", None),
            "pr_review_job_created",
            actor=request_actor(request),
            details={"job_id": result.job_id, "repo": req.repo, "number": req.number},
            audit_log_path=request.app.state.config.audit_log_path,
        )
        job = await jobs.get(result.job_id)
        return job or {"job_id": result.job_id}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Add the CLI command**

In `embry0/cli/__main__.py`, after `cmd_onboard`:

```python
def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Handle ``embry0 review <repo> <number>`` (#30).

    Thin API client: POST /api/v1/jobs {pipeline: "pr-review"}. Reviews one
    pull request now, whether or not the repo has review turned on; every
    other gate (embry0's own pull request, budget) still applies. Prints the
    job and does not wait: the review lands on the pull request itself.
    """
    import httpx

    base = (args.api_url or os.environ.get("EMBRY0_API_URL") or "http://localhost:8200").rstrip("/")
    key = _resolve_api_key(args.api_key)
    headers = {"X-Requested-With": "XMLHttpRequest"}
    if key:
        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {key}"
    try:
        resp = httpx.post(
            f"{base}/api/v1/jobs",
            json={"pipeline": "pr-review", "repo": args.repo, "number": args.number},
            headers=headers,
            timeout=httpx.Timeout(60.0, connect=10.0),
        )
    except httpx.ConnectError:
        _err(f"cannot reach the orchestrator at {base} — is the stack running? (embry0 start)")
        sys.exit(2)
    if resp.status_code == 401:
        _err("orchestrator rejected the request (401) — set EMBRY0_API_KEY or pass --api-key")
        sys.exit(2)
    if resp.status_code >= 400:
        _err(f"review not started ({resp.status_code}): {resp.text[:500]}")
        sys.exit(2)
    job_id = resp.json().get("job_id")
    _info(f"review job {job_id} started for {args.repo}#{args.number}; follow it at {base}/jobs/{job_id}")
```

```bash
feat(api,cli): wire pr-review jobs into the executor

The executor now runs pr-review jobs like onboard ones: budget gate
first, then the graph, on the repo's model override or the heavy tier,
with no env vars for a fork. A job that began is failed rather than
resumed after a restart, one that never began is launched again, and
its sandbox is never enrolled to push.

POST /api/v1/jobs takes pipeline pr-review with a number, and
`embry0 review <repo> <number>` calls it. Startup seeds the reviewer
agent and wires the dispatcher the webhooks use.
```

```tsx
import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import { render, screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";

const mockUseRepoPreferences = vi.fn();
const mockMutate = vi.fn();

vi.mock("@/hooks/useRepoPreferences", () => ({
  useRepoPreferences: (...a: unknown[]) => mockUseRepoPreferences(...a),
  useSetRepoPreferences: () => ({ mutate: mockMutate, isPending: false }),
}));
vi.mock("@/hooks/useModels", () => ({
  useModels: () => ({ models: ["claude-opus-5", "claude-sonnet-5"], isLoading: false }),
}));
vi.mock("sonner", () => ({ toast: { success: vi.fn(), error: vi.fn() } }));

import { PullRequestReviewCard } from "../PullRequestReviewCard";

const BASE = {
  repo: "acme/widgets",
  sandbox_profile: null,
  language_hint: null,
  notes: "",
  execution_mode: null,
  auth_mode: null,
  git_author_name: null,
  git_author_email: null,
  dev_checks: [],
  base_branch: null,
  updated_at: "2026-10-15T00:00:00Z",
};

function withPrReview(pr_review: unknown) {
  mockUseRepoPreferences.mockReturnValue({ data: { ...BASE, pr_review }, isLoading: false });
}

describe("PullRequestReviewCard", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks();
    withPrReview(null);
  });

  it("is off, full depth and on the heavy tier for a repo that never configured it", () => {
    render(<PullRequestReviewCard repo="acme/widgets" />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("switch", { name: "Review pull requests" })).toHaveAttribute("aria-checked", "false");
    expect(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: /^Full/ })).toBeChecked();
    const model = screen.getByLabelText("Model");
    expect(model).toHaveValue("");
    expect(within(model).getAllByRole("option")[0]).toHaveTextContent("Default (heavy tier)");
  });

  it("describes each depth on its own line", () => {
    render(<PullRequestReviewCard repo="acme/widgets" />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/run every dev check/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/whose paths the pull request touches/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Pull requests from forks are always reviewed this way/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("shows the stored settings", () => {
    withPrReview({ enabled: true, depth: "read_only", model: "claude-opus-5" });
    render(<PullRequestReviewCard repo="acme/widgets" />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("switch", { name: "Review pull requests" })).toHaveAttribute("aria-checked", "true");
    expect(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: /^Read only/ })).toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Model")).toHaveValue("claude-opus-5");
  });

  it("saves the whole settings object and nothing else", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    render(<PullRequestReviewCard repo="acme/widgets" />);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("switch", { name: "Review pull requests" }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: /^Targeted/ }));
    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText("Model"), "claude-sonnet-5");
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save review settings" }));

    await waitFor(() => expect(mockMutate).toHaveBeenCalled());
    const { fullRepo, update } = mockMutate.mock.calls[0][0];
    expect(fullRepo).toBe("acme/widgets");
    expect(update).toEqual({ pr_review: { enabled: true, depth: "targeted", model: "claude-sonnet-5" } });
  });

  it("sends null for the default model", async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    withPrReview({ enabled: true, depth: "full", model: "claude-opus-5" });
    render(<PullRequestReviewCard repo="acme/widgets" />);
    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText("Model"), "");
    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save review settings" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(mockMutate).toHaveBeenCalled());
    expect(mockMutate.mock.calls[0][0].update.pr_review.model).toBeNull();
  });

  it("keeps Save disabled until something changes", () => {
    render(<PullRequestReviewCard repo="acme/widgets" />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save review settings" })).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it("lists the webhook events the repository needs", () => {
    render(<PullRequestReviewCard repo="acme/widgets" />);
    const note = screen.getByTestId("pr-review-webhook-note");
    for (const event of ["Pull requests", "Issue comments", "Merge request events", "Comments"]) {
      expect(note).toHaveTextContent(event);
    }
  });
});
```

```ts
/** #30: how deep a pull request review goes. Forks are always read_only. */
export type PrReviewDepth = "full" | "targeted" | "read_only";

/** #30: per-repo pull request review settings. `model: null` = the heavy tier. */
export interface PrReviewSettings {
  enabled: boolean;
  depth: PrReviewDepth;
  model: string | null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the card**

Create `frontend/src/components/environments/PullRequestReviewCard.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/Card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/Button";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/Label";
import { Select } from "@/components/ui/Select";
import { useRepoPreferences, useSetRepoPreferences } from "@/hooks/useRepoPreferences";
import { useModels } from "@/hooks/useModels";
import type { PrReviewDepth, PrReviewSettings } from "@/api/repoPreferences";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const DEFAULTS: PrReviewSettings = { enabled: false, depth: "full", model: null };

const DEPTH_OPTIONS: { value: PrReviewDepth; label: string; description: string }[] = [
  {
    value: "full",
    label: "Full",
    description:
      "Install dependencies and run every dev check. With none configured, the reviewer works out the commands from the repository and suggests them.",
  },
  {
    value: "targeted",
    label: "Targeted",
    description: "Run only the dev checks whose paths the pull request touches.",
  },
  {
    value: "read_only",
    label: "Read only",
    description: "Read the code and the diff, and run nothing. Pull requests from forks are always reviewed this way.",
  },
];

interface PullRequestReviewCardProps {
  /** Full repo path; may exceed two segments (GitLab subgroups, #162). */
  repo: string;
}

/** #30: per-repo pull request review settings, saved as one object. */
export function PullRequestReviewCard({ repo }: PullRequestReviewCardProps) {
  const { data: prefs, isLoading } = useRepoPreferences(repo);
  const { models } = useModels();
  const setPrefs = useSetRepoPreferences();
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<PrReviewSettings>(DEFAULTS);
  const [dirty, setDirty] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSettings({ ...DEFAULTS, ...(prefs?.pr_review ?? {}) });
    setDirty(false);
  }, [prefs]);

  const update = (patch: Partial<PrReviewSettings>) => {
    setSettings((current) => ({ ...current, ...patch }));
    setDirty(true);
  };

  const handleSave = () => {
    setPrefs.mutate(
      // PUT is a partial update (#350): only pr_review is sent, so the other
      // preferences are kept.
      { fullRepo: repo, update: { pr_review: settings } },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          toast.success("Pull request review settings saved");
          setDirty(false);
        },
        onError: (e: Error) => toast.error(`Failed: ${e.message}`),
      },
    );
  };

  return (
    <Card className="p-4 space-y-4" data-testid="pr-review-card">
      <div>
        <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold">Pull request review</h3>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">
          When a pull request becomes ready for review, embry0 reviews it and posts its findings as comments. It
          never approves a pull request and never blocks a merge.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <button
          type="button"
          role="switch"
          aria-checked={settings.enabled}
          aria-label="Review pull requests"
          disabled={isLoading}
          onClick={() => update({ enabled: !settings.enabled })}
          className={cn(
            "relative inline-flex h-5 w-9 shrink-0 items-center rounded-full transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring",
            settings.enabled ? "bg-cyan-500" : "bg-white/20",
          )}
        >
          <span
            className={cn(
              "inline-block h-4 w-4 rounded-full bg-white transition-transform",
              settings.enabled ? "translate-x-4" : "translate-x-0.5",
            )}
          />
        </button>
        <span className="text-sm">{settings.enabled ? "On" : "Off"}</span>
      </div>

      <fieldset className="space-y-2">
        <legend className="text-sm font-medium">Depth</legend>
        {DEPTH_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
          <label key={option.value} className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm cursor-pointer">
            <input
              type="radio"
              name="pr-review-depth"
              value={option.value}
              checked={settings.depth === option.value}
              onChange={() => update({ depth: option.value })}
              className="mt-1"
            />
            <span>
              <span className="font-medium">{option.label}</span>
              <span className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">{option.description}</span>
            </span>
          </label>
        ))}
      </fieldset>

      <div className="space-y-1">
        <Label htmlFor="pr-review-model">Model</Label>
        <Select
          id="pr-review-model"
          value={settings.model ?? ""}
          onChange={(e) => update({ model: e.target.value || null })}
        >
          <option value="">Default (heavy tier)</option>
          {models.map((m) => (
            <option key={m} value={m}>
              {m}
            </option>
          ))}
        </Select>
      </div>

      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground" data-testid="pr-review-webhook-note">
        The repository's webhook must send these events. GitHub: <strong>Pull requests</strong> and{" "}
        <strong>Issue comments</strong>. GitLab: <strong>Merge request events</strong> and <strong>Comments</strong>.
        Anyone with write access can ask for another review with a comment line that reads{" "}
        <code>/embry0 review</code>.
      </p>

      <Button size="sm" onClick={handleSave} disabled={setPrefs.isPending || !dirty}>
        {setPrefs.isPending ? "Saving…" : "Save review settings"}
      </Button>
    </Card>
  );
}
```

`cn` lives in `@/lib/utils` (the `Card` and `Select` components import it from there).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the end-to-end test**

Create `tests/unit/workflows/pr_review/test_end_to_end.py`:

```python
"""One pull request, reviewed from webhook to publish, then re-reviewed on a command (#30).

Real: the webhook route, PrReviewDispatcher, IssueExecutor, the pr-review
graph, run_agent_node and every repository, against Postgres. Stubbed: the
sandbox, its docker exec, the agent, GitHub's REST API and the checkpointer.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from typing import Any
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, patch

import httpx
import pytest
from httpx import ASGITransport, AsyncClient
from langgraph.checkpoint.memory import InMemorySaver

from embry0.api.app import create_app
from embry0.config import Embry0Config
from embry0.execution.agent_runner import AgentOutput
from embry0.execution.github_client import GitHubClient
from embry0.services.issue_executor import IssueExecutor
from embry0.services.pr_review.dispatcher import PrReviewDispatcher
from embry0.storage.repositories.agent_definitions import AgentDefinitionsRepository
from embry0.storage.repositories.agent_sessions import AgentSessionsRepository
from embry0.storage.repositories.issues import IssuesRepository
from embry0.storage.repositories.jobs import JobsRepository
from embry0.storage.repositories.pr_reviews import PrReviewsRepository
from embry0.storage.repositories.repo_preferences import RepoPreferencesRepository
from embry0.storage.repositories.repos import ReposRepository
from embry0.storage.repositories.sandbox_profiles import SandboxProfilesRepository
from embry0.storage.repositories.traces import TracesRepository
from embry0.storage.seeds.sandbox_profiles_builtin import seed_builtin_sandbox_profiles
from embry0.workflows.pr_review.agent_seed import seed_pr_reviewer_agent
from embry0.workflows.pr_review.checkout import DELTA_DIFF, FILES_LIST
from embry0.workflows.pr_review.summary import review_marker
from embry0.workflows.registry import WorkflowRegistry
from tests.fixtures.webhooks import github_issue_comment, github_review_pull_request

pytestmark = [pytest.mark.requires_postgres]

SHA = "a" * 40
SHA_2 = "b" * 40
PATCH = "@@ -1,2 +1,3 @@\n a = 1\n+b = 2\n c = 3"
FIRST = {
    "summary": "One unhandled error.",
    "review_comments": [{"file": "src/app.py", "line": 2, "severity": "important", "comment": "b is never checked."}],
    "checks_run": [{"name": "unit", "command": "pytest -q", "status": "passed", "output_tail": ""}],
    "suggested_dev_checks": [],
    "previous_findings": [],
}
SECOND = {
    "summary": "The earlier finding is fixed.",
    "review_comments": [],
    "checks_run": [{"name": "unit", "command": "pytest -q", "status": "passed", "output_tail": ""}],
    "suggested_dev_checks": [],
    "previous_findings": [{"file": "src/app.py", "line": 2, "comment": "b is never checked.", "status": "resolved"}],
}


class _Docker:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.scripts: list[str] = []

    def build_exec_cmd(self, cid: str, cmd: list[str]) -> list[str]:
        return ["docker", "exec", cid, *cmd]

    async def run_cmd(self, cmd: list[str], timeout: int = 60, **_kw: Any) -> str:
        if "embry0-review-checkout:" in cmd[-1]:
            self.scripts.append(cmd[-1])
            delta = "yes" if "prev=" in cmd[-1] else "none"
            return f"embry0-review-checkout: delta={delta} base={'c' * 40}"
        if cmd[-2:] == ["cat", FILES_LIST]:
            return "src/app.py\n"
        return ""


class _Sandbox:
    def __init__(self, docker: _Docker) -> None:
        self.runtime = type("Runtime", (), {"client": docker})()
        self.destroyed: list[str] = []

    def _read_oauth_token(self) -> str:
        return "oauth-test-token"

    async def create(self, job_id: str, **_kw: Any) -> tuple[str, str]:
        return f"sandbox-{job_id}", "tok"

    async def destroy(self, container_id: str) -> None:
        self.destroyed.append(container_id)


class _Runner:
    def __init__(self, outputs: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
        self.outputs = list(outputs)
        self.calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    async def run(self, container: str, config: dict[str, Any], network: Any = None, on_event: Any = None, *, resume_session: Any = None) -> AgentOutput:
        self.calls.append({"config": config, "resume_session": resume_session})
        return AgentOutput(
            agent_type="pr-reviewer",
            output=json.dumps(self.outputs.pop(0)),
            session_id=f"sess-{len(self.calls)}",
            messages=[{"role": "assistant", "content": "reviewed"}],
        )


class _Forge:
    """GitHub's REST API for one pull request whose head can move."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.head = SHA
        self.reviews: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    def client(self) -> GitHubClient:
        async def _sleep(_seconds: float) -> None:
            return None

        return GitHubClient(default_token="t", http_client=httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.MockTransport(self.handle)), sleep=_sleep)

    def handle(self, request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        path = request.url.path
        if request.method == "GET" and path == "/repos/acme/widgets/pulls/7":
            return httpx.Response(200, json=github_review_pull_request(head_sha=self.head)["pull_request"])
        if request.method == "GET" and path == "/repos/acme/widgets/pulls/7/files":
            return httpx.Response(200, json=[{"filename": "src/app.py", "status": "modified", "patch": PATCH}])
        if request.method == "POST" and path == "/repos/acme/widgets/pulls/7/reviews":
            self.reviews.append(json.loads(request.content))
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"id": len(self.reviews)})
        return httpx.Response(404, json={"message": f"unexpected {request.method} {path}"})


@asynccontextmanager
async def _memory_checkpointer(_url: str):
    yield InMemorySaver()


async def _finished(jobs: JobsRepository, job_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    for _ in range(300):
        row = await jobs.get(job_id)
        if row is not None and row["status"] in ("completed", "failed"):
            return row
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
    pytest.fail(f"job {job_id} never finished")


async def test_a_ready_pull_request_is_reviewed_then_re_reviewed_on_a_command(db_with_migrations) -> None:
    db = db_with_migrations
    repos = ReposRepository(db)
    prefs = RepoPreferencesRepository(db)
    jobs = JobsRepository(db)
    await repos.register("acme/widgets", forge="github")
    await prefs.upsert(
        "acme/widgets",
        pr_review={"enabled": True, "depth": "full", "model": None},
        dev_checks=[{"name": "unit", "paths": [], "command": "pytest -q"}],
    )
    await seed_builtin_sandbox_profiles(SandboxProfilesRepository(db))
    await seed_pr_reviewer_agent(AgentDefinitionsRepository(db))

    docker = _Docker()
    sandbox = _Sandbox(docker)
    runner = _Runner([FIRST, SECOND])
    forge = _Forge()
    config = Embry0Config(_env_file=None, auth_dev_mode=True, webhook_dev_mode=True, github_webhook_secret="")
    executor = IssueExecutor(
        issues_repo=IssuesRepository(db),
        jobs_repo=jobs,
        traces_repo=TracesRepository(db),
        workflow_registry=WorkflowRegistry(),
        database_url="postgresql://unused/unused",
        db=db,
        config=config,
        sandbox_manager=sandbox,
        agent_runner=runner,
        repo_preferences_repo=prefs,
        repos_repo=repos,
        profiles_repo=SandboxProfilesRepository(db),
        agent_sessions_repo=AgentSessionsRepository(db),
    )
    app = create_app(config)
    app.state.pr_review_dispatcher = PrReviewDispatcher(
        config=config,
        repos_repo=repos,
        prefs_repo=prefs,
        jobs_repo=jobs,
        reviews_repo=PrReviewsRepository(db),
        executor=executor,
        github=forge.client(),
        gitlab=None,
    )

    async def deliver(event: str, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        async with AsyncClient(transport=ASGITransport(app=app), base_url="http://test") as client:
            resp = await client.post(
                "/api/v1/webhook",
                content=json.dumps(payload).encode(),
                headers={"X-GitHub-Event": event, "Content-Type": "application/json"},
            )
        assert resp.status_code == 200, resp.text
        return dict(resp.json())

    with (
        patch("embry0.services._executor_run.checkpointer_context", _memory_checkpointer),
        patch("embry0.services.issue_executor.emit_audit", new=AsyncMock()),
        patch("embry0.workflows.pr_review.nodes._github_client", side_effect=lambda _c: forge.client()),
    ):
        # 1. Marked ready → a review lands on the pull request.
        first = await deliver("pull_request", github_review_pull_request())
        first_job = first["pr_review"]["job_id"]
        row = await _finished(jobs, first_job)
        assert row["status"] == "completed", (row["error_code"], row["error_message"])
        assert row["result_summary"] == "One unhandled error."
        assert row["pr_url"] is None, "a review job must not claim the pull request"

        [review] = forge.reviews
        assert (review["event"], review["commit_id"]) == ("COMMENT", SHA)
        assert [(c["path"], c["line"], c["side"]) for c in review["comments"]] == [("src/app.py", 2, "RIGHT")]
        assert review["comments"][0]["body"].startswith("**Important** — b is never checked.")
        assert "**passed** `unit`: `pytest -q`" in review["body"]
        assert review["body"].rstrip().endswith(review_marker(first_job, SHA))
        assert "- unit: `pytest -q`" in runner.calls[0]["config"]["prompt"]
        assert sandbox.destroyed == [f"sandbox-{first_job}"]

        # 2. A redelivery of the same event is refused, durably.
        again = await deliver("pull_request", github_review_pull_request())
        assert again["pr_review"] == {"dispatched": False, "reason": "duplicate"}

        # 3. A push, then a command: the review continues the first one's session.
        forge.head = SHA_2
        comment = github_issue_comment(body="Fixed it.\n/embry0 review", repo="acme/widgets", number=7, on_pull_request=True)
        second = await deliver("issue_comment", comment)
        assert second["action"] == "pr_review" and second["dispatched"] is True, second
        second_job = second["job_id"]
        row = await _finished(jobs, second_job)
        assert row["status"] == "completed", (row["error_code"], row["error_message"])

    resumed = runner.calls[1]
    assert resumed["resume_session"] is not None and resumed["resume_session"].session_id == "sess-1", (
        "the re-review must continue the first review's session"
    )
    assert DELTA_DIFF in resumed["config"]["prompt"] and SHA[:12] in resumed["config"]["prompt"]
    assert f"prev={SHA}" in docker.scripts[1]
    assert len(forge.reviews) == 2
    assert forge.reviews[1]["commit_id"] == SHA_2
    assert "resolved — `src/app.py:2` b is never checked." in forge.reviews[1]["body"]

    rows = await db.fetch("SELECT job_id, trigger, posted_at FROM pr_reviews ORDER BY created_at")
    assert [(r["job_id"], r["trigger"]) for r in rows] == [(first_job, "ready"), (second_job, "command")]
    assert all(r["posted_at"] is not None for r in rows)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `docs/pr-review.md`**

Create `docs/pr-review.md`:

````markdown
# Pull request review

embry0 can review pull requests (GitHub) and merge requests (GitLab) on a registered repository and post what it finds on them. It is an **advisory reviewer**: it comments, and it never approves, never requests changes and sets no status check. The author and the human reviewers decide.

Review is **off** for every repository until an operator turns it on (Repositories → the repository → *Pull request review*, or `PUT /api/v1/repos/{repo}/preferences` with a `pr_review` object).

## When a review runs

| Forge | Event | Starts a review when |
|---|---|---|
| GitHub | `pull_request` | `ready_for_review`, or `opened` with `draft: false` |
| GitHub | `issue_comment` | on a pull request, a line of the comment starts with `/embry0 review` |
| GitLab | Merge Request Hook | the draft flag goes from true to false (`draft`, or `work_in_progress` on older GitLab), or `open` as non-draft |
| GitLab | Note Hook | on a merge request, a line of the note starts with `/embry0 review` |

Pushes do not start a review. The command is matched case-insensitively at the start of a line, and not inside a code block or a `>` quote. It is not the `@embry0` follow-up tag: a comment with only `@embry0` never starts a review, and a `/embry0 review` comment is never also a follow-up.

From the command line, `embry0 review <owner/repo> <number>` reviews one pull request now, whether or not the repository has review turned on (the other gates still apply).

A trigger is refused, with the reason in the orchestrator log (`pr_review_refused`) and nothing posted, when:

1. the repository is not registered, or its review is off (the CLI skips the on/off setting only);
2. the pull request is embry0's own (a job opened it, or its branch starts with `embry0/`);
3. a comment's author lacks write access — on GitHub an `author_association` outside `COMMENT_DISPATCH_AUTHOR_ROLES` (default `OWNER,MEMBER,COLLABORATOR`) or a bot; on GitLab a project access level below Developer (30);
4. the same trigger was seen before: a ready event is keyed on its pull request and head sha, a command on its comment id (`pr_reviews.trigger_key`, unique, so this survives restarts);
5. the budget gate refuses the actor — the pull request's author for a ready event, the commenter for a command.

## What the review does

Each review is a `pr-review` job, visible on the dashboard, with its own sandbox (the repository's sandbox profile, else `slim`). The sandbox clones the base branch through the git-proxy and checks out the exact head commit the trigger named; a fork's head is fetched from the base repository (`refs/pull/<n>/head`, `refs/merge-requests/<iid>/head`). If that commit is gone — the branch was force-pushed after the trigger — the job fails with `ERR_PR_REVIEW_INIT_FAILED` and nothing is posted.

The `pr-reviewer` agent (seeded at startup, editable under Agents) runs on the repository's model, or the deployment's heavy tier (`MODEL_HEAVY`). Its run is limited to 60 minutes, and each check it runs to 20.

| Depth | What runs |
|---|---|
| `full` (default) | Installs dependencies and runs every `dev_checks` entry. With none configured, the agent works out the commands from the repository's CI configuration, Makefile, `package.json`, `pyproject.toml` and README, says what it ran, and suggests them as `dev_checks`. |
| `targeted` | Only the `dev_checks` entries whose paths the change touches (an entry with no paths always runs). |
| `read_only` | Nothing: the agent reads the code and the diff, and has no shell. |

## What gets posted

The orchestrator posts with the repository's forge credential; nothing is posted from the sandbox.

- **Inline comments** on the lines a finding names, when that line is part of the diff: at most 30, worst first, each starting with **Critical**, **Important** or **Suggestion**. A finding anywhere else — outside the diff, a binary or deleted file, past the 30 — is listed in the summary under *Elsewhere in the code*. When someone pushed between the review and the publish, nothing is placed inline, since the lines belong to another commit.
- **A summary**: the assessment and counts by severity; the checks run, with the output tail of any that failed (for a fork: *Checks skipped: this pull request comes from a fork*); on a re-review, each earlier finding marked resolved or still open; suggested `dev_checks` as a block to paste; and a footer with the reviewed sha, a link to the job and how to ask for another review.

GitHub gets one review (`event: COMMENT`); if GitHub refuses it (422), it is posted once more with every finding in the summary. GitLab gets a diff discussion per inline finding (a position GitLab refuses moves into the summary) and one summary note. A forge error after that fails the job with `ERR_PR_REVIEW_PUBLISH_FAILED` and the forge's message. An unreadable verdict fails it with `ERR_PR_REVIEW_NO_VERDICT`. Either way the verdict is kept in `pr_reviews.verdict`, and `pr_reviews.posted_at` says whether the review landed.

## Re-reviews

A `/embry0 review` comment continues the agent session of the pull request's last posted review: the agent is told what changed since the sha it last reviewed, re-checks its earlier findings, and reviews the new commits. When the branch was rewritten since, it reviews the whole change again and still reports its earlier findings. When there is no session to continue (the first review, a session too large to resume, or a purged job), it starts fresh with the earlier findings in its prompt.

## Forks and the trust model

A pull request from a fork is always reviewed read-only, whatever the depth: no command runs, the agent has no shell, and the sandbox gets none of the repository's environment variables. Code a job runs can read its sandbox's credentials, including the LLM credential ([ADR 0008](adr/0008-llm-credential-in-sandbox-env.md)), so running a stranger's tests would hand them that credential. The pull request's description and code reach the agent as data to review; the agent is told that an instruction found in them is a finding, not an instruction.

## Webhook setup

Subscribe the repository's webhook to these events (see [webhooks](webhooks.md)):

- GitHub: **Pull requests** and **Issue comments**.
- GitLab: **Merge request events** and **Comments**.

No new route: reviews ride the existing `/api/v1/webhook` and `/api/v1/webhook/gitlab`, so what each ingress exposes is unchanged.

## Spend

Each review is a job: it passes the dispatch budget gate (daily and monthly caps, the per-author hourly limit) before it is created and again before it runs, and its spend counts against the per-job cap like any other job's.
````

```markdown
**Pull request review (#30).** With review turned on for the repository, `pull_request` (`ready_for_review`, or `opened` as non-draft) and an `issue_comment` on a pull request with a line starting `/embry0 review` start an advisory review that embry0 posts on the pull request. The `pull_request` events also keep feeding merge sync, as before. See [pull request review](pr-review.md).
```

GitLab section, replace the **Trigger** bullet with:


In `docs/glossary.md`, add after the `### Delivery state` entry:

```markdown
### Pull request review
The `pr-review` pipeline (#30, `docs/pr-review.md`): an advisory review of someone's pull request or merge request, posted on it as a summary plus inline comments, never an approval or a block. It starts when a pull request becomes ready (or opens as non-draft) on a repository whose `repo_preferences.pr_review.enabled` is on, or on a `/embry0 review` comment from someone with write access; a re-review continues the previous review's agent session. One row per review in `pr_reviews`, whose UNIQUE `trigger_key` makes a redelivered webhook a no-op. A fork is always reviewed read-only. (2026-10)
```

and in the Agents section after `### Reviewer`:

```markdown
### PR reviewer
The `pr-reviewer` agent of the [pull request review](#pull-request-review) pipeline. Unlike the [Reviewer](#reviewer), which judges the developer agent's change inside issue-to-pr, it reviews a change someone else made, reports findings without a decision, and runs the repository's `dev_checks` (or works them out) at the repository's review depth. (2026-10)
```

1. `browser_navigate` to `http://localhost:3001/`, then `browser_run_code_unsafe` with:

```js
async (page) => {
  await page.route("**/api/v1/**", async (route) => {
    const request = route.request();
    const url = new URL(request.url());
    const json = (body) => route.fulfill({ status: 200, contentType: "application/json", body: JSON.stringify(body) });
    if (url.pathname.endsWith("/repos/acme/widgets/preferences")) {
      if (request.method() === "PUT") {
        const body = request.postDataJSON();
        await page.evaluate((b) => { window.__lastPreferencesPut = b; }, body);
        return json({ repo: "acme/widgets", notes: "", dev_checks: [], updated_at: "2026-10-15T00:00:00Z", ...body });
      }
      return json(null);
    }
    if (url.pathname.endsWith("/repos/acme/widgets")) {
      return json({
        repo: "acme/widgets", active: true, notes: "", created_at: "2026-10-15T00:00:00Z",
        updated_at: "2026-10-15T00:00:00Z", forge: "github", has_qa_config: false, docker_access: false,
        preferences: null, env_var_count: 0, env_secret_count: 0,
      });
    }
    if (url.pathname.endsWith("/config/models")) {
      return json({ heavy: "claude-opus-5", medium: "claude-sonnet-5", light: "claude-haiku-4-5", providers: [] });
    }
    return json([]);
  });
  await page.goto("http://localhost:3001/repos/acme/widgets");
  await page.getByRole("heading", { name: "Pull request review" }).waitFor();
  return "ready";
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Push and open the pull request (ask Tomas first)**

Pushing touches shared state, so confirm with Tomas before running:

```bash
git push -u origin feat/pr-review
gh pr create --base main --head feat/pr-review --title "feat: review pull requests when they are ready" --body-file - <<'BODY'
## What

embry0 reviews a pull request (GitHub) or merge request (GitLab) on a registered repo when it becomes ready for review, or when someone with write access comments `/embry0 review`, and posts an advisory review on it: a summary plus at most 30 inline comments, worst first. It never approves, never requests changes and sets no status check. A re-review continues the previous review's agent session and reports which earlier findings were resolved.

- Triggers and the five gates (enabled, not embry0's own, author write access, durable dedupe, budget): `embry0/services/pr_review/`.
- The `pr-review` pipeline (`init → review → publish → cleanup`) and the `pr-reviewer` agent: `embry0/workflows/pr_review/`.
- Settings: `repo_preferences.pr_review` (`enabled`, `depth`: full | targeted | read_only, `model`), edited in the dashboard's new "Pull request review" card. Off by default.
- Manual trigger: `embry0 review <repo> <number>` (`POST /api/v1/jobs {pipeline: "pr-review"}`).
- Forks are always reviewed read-only: no commands, no shell, no env vars.

Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-pr-review-design.md`. Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-15-pr-review.md`. Operator doc: `docs/pr-review.md`.

## Verification

- Full unit suite against a throwaway Postgres; failure set identical to `origin/main` (paste both summary lines).
- `ruff check`, `ruff format --check`, `mypy embry0`; frontend `tsc -b`, lint, vitest, build.
- End-to-end test: ready event → review posted → redelivery refused → push + `/embry0 review` → resumed review posted (`tests/unit/workflows/pr_review/test_end_to_end.py`).
- Checkout script exercised against real git repositories, including a force-pushed branch and a head that is gone.
- Playwright check of the settings card (screenshots attached).

## Deploy notes

- Orchestrator and frontend through `infra/deploy.sh`. Migration **95** (`pr_reviews`, `repo_preferences.pr_review`) runs at startup; renumber to max+1 if another migration merges first.
- No sandbox or proxy image rebuild: nothing under `embry0/sandbox/`, `embry0/safety/`, `embry0/agents/` or `embry0/execution/proxy/` changed.
- No new route; what each ingress exposes is unchanged.
- To use it on a repo: turn on *Pull request review* in its preferences, and subscribe its webhook to **Pull requests** and **Issue comments** (GitHub) or **Merge request events** and **Comments** (GitLab).

Closes #30

https://claude.ai/code/session_01Q5pSvJSg6T12CagYZMAHYy
BODY
```
